kāṇikki koṭuppar. cīriya kiṟittavar tamatu kuḻantikaḷukkuc cātakameḻuti vippar. tirumaṇattil maṇa makaṉ peṇṇukkut tāli tarippāṉ. kaṇavaṉ maraṇamāṉāl maṉivi tāliyik kaḷintu viṭuvāḷ. maraṇattukkup piṉ pattu allatu patiṉintu nāṭkaḷukkup pular eṉṉum tīṭṭuk kāppārkaḷ. āṇṭil oru muṟi iṟanta nāḷ koṇṭāṭappaṭum.
cūli: kaṉṉaṭat tēvatācikaḷ ip peyar peṟuvar.
cekkaṉ: vaṉṉiyaṉukku itu maṟṟoru peyar.
cekkāṉ: ivarkaḷ teṉmaliyāḷattil vaṭṭakkāṭar eṉavum vaṭamaliyāḷattil vāṇiyar eṉavum paṭuvar. cekkār vāṇiyarilum pārkka uyarntavarkaḷāvar. cekkār tīṇṭuvatāl nāyarukkut tīṭṭu uṇṭu. ivarkaḷ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷ nāyar cātiyaṉa ruṭiyavi pōlvaṉa.
ceṭṭi: necavukārar, cekkār mutaliyōr itaṉit tamakkup paṭṭap peyarākak koḷvar. pēricceṭṭi, nakarac ceṭṭi, kācukkārac ceṭṭi, nāṭṭuk kōṭṭic ceṭṭi mutaliyōr ceṭṭi vakuppiṟ cilarāvar. (ceṭṭikaḷ tammit taṉaviciyar eṉak kūṟuvar. taṉavici yariṉ pirivukaḷ kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattuc ceṭṭi, ēḻilic ceṭṭi, iḷiyāttuc kuṭicceṭṭi, cōḻapurattuc ceṭṭi, puḷiyaṅkuṭic ceṭṭi, pūvaḷḷuk kuṭic ceṭṭi, tiruvāppūrc ceṭṭi, karuppūrac ceṭṭi, kāvēric ceṭṭi, vaḷiyal ceṭṭi, mañcappaṭṭuc ceṭṭi eṉac cimaṉ kācic ceṭṭiyavarkaḷ kuṟippiṭuvar.)
cempaṭavaṉ: tamiḻnāṭṭil mīṉ piṭikārar cempaṭavarāvar. ivarkaḷ nāṭṭāṉ, kavaṇṭaṉ, maṇiyakkāraṉ, pākuttaṉ, piḷḷi mutaliya paṭṭappeyari vittukkoḷvar. maliyaṉūril ellāc cempaṭavarum tammip pūcārikaḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkuvar. citamparattilē kuṟikkappaṭṭa oru nāḷil cempaṭavar cuvāmiyic cumantu ūrvalam varuvar. ataṟkāka avarkaḷukkuk kūliyum poṅkalum koṭukkappaṭum. ammaṉ ūrvalam varumpōtu cempaṭavar ammaṉi niṟutti āṭi koṭuppārkaḷ. avarkaḷ talivaṉ nāṭṭāṇmikkāraṉ eṉappaṭu vāṉ. avaṉuṭiya utavikkāraṉ caṅkatip piḷḷi allatu caṅkatik kāraṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. paṭaku ōṭṭum cempaṭavar kaṅkiyi vaḻipaṭuvar. pūppaṭinta peṇ kāvalākak kiyil irumpi vittiruppāḷ. peṇ karuppamāki ēḻāvatu mātam mutukunīr kuttal eṉṉum caṭaṅku naṭattappaṭum. peṇ kuṉintu niṟkumpōtu uṟaviṉar veṟṟiliyiṉ nuṉiyāl avaḷ mutukiṉ mītu pāl vārppārkaḷ.
cemmāṉ: ivarkaḷ paṟiyaril oru piriviṉar; tōlil vēli ceyvōr. ippoḻutu ivarkaḷ ceyyum vēliyic cakkiliyar purikiṉṟaṉar.
cerumāṉ: ivarkaḷ maliyāḷattil uḻavuttoḻil ceyyum vēliyāḷar. vaṭa maliyāḷattil ivarkaḷ puliyar eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ kāṇiyāḷarāl aṭimikaḷāy viṟkavum vāṅkavum paṭṭārkaḷ. 1862il ivvāṟu viṟṟu vāṅkutal caṭṭa virōtamāk kappaṭṭatu. ivarkaḷ cātimāṉkaḷukku 30 aṭi tūrattil vantāl tīṭṭu uṇṭākum. cerumār, pirāmaṇar kirāmaṅkaḷ, kōyilkaḷi aṇukutal kūṭātu. iṉṟum cerumār cantikaḷuḷ nuḻital kūṭātu. ivarkaḷ pirāmaṇarukkum nāyarukkum aṟupattu nāṉkaṭi tūrattil niṟṟal vēṇṭum.
cēṇiyaṉ: necavu toḻilāḷar cēṇiyareṉappaṭuvar. kāñcī purattil cēṇiyār iliṅka matattiṉarāvar.
cēṉikkuṭiyār: ivarkaḷ veṟṟili payiriṭuvar. ivarkaḷ ili eṉavum, koṭikkāṟ piḷḷikaḷ eṉavum aṟiyappaṭuvar. mūppaṉ, piḷḷi eṉṉum paṭṭap peyarkaḷum ivarkaḷuḷ vaḻaṅkum. ivarkaḷ vīṭukaḷil paṟiyar, ampaṭṭar, vaṇṇār uṇṇamāṭ ṭārkaḷ.
coṇṭi: ōriya vakuppiṉaruḷ kaḷ viṟpōr ivarkaḷāvar. ivarkaḷ arici, paṉaṅkaṭṭi, paṉaṅkaḷ, iruppip pū mutaliyavaṟṟi liruntu cārāyam vaṭippar. talip pūppu eytiya peṇ, nāṉku ampukaḷi naṭṭuk kayiṟṟāṟ ṟoṭuttuk kaṭṭappaṭṭa iṭattil viṭappaṭuvāḷ. ēḻāvatu nāḷ avaḷukku muḻukkāṭṭappaṭum. pūppaṭiyumuṉ peṇkaḷukku maṇamākum. iṟantavarkaḷ putikkappaṭu vārkaḷ. maraṇattīṭṭup pattu nāṭkaḷukkuṇṭu. peṇkaḷ macca māmicam uṇṇārkaḷ. kañcam pakutiyil peṇkaḷ pūppaṭinta piṉ maṇappar.
cōlakar: ivarkaḷ koccik kaṉṉaṭa moḻi pēcum makkaḷ.
cōḻiyappaṭṭar: itu paṭṭap pirāmaṇa vakuppiṉarukku maliyāḷattil vaḻaṅkum peyar.
cōṉakar: ivarkaḷ intut tāy tantiyar marapil vanta mucalmāṉkaḷāvar. cōṉakaṉ eṉṉum peyar arāpiyaṉik kuṟikkum. maliyāḷattu māppiḷḷimār cōṉaka māppiḷḷimār eṉappaṭuvar. kirēkkarik kuṟikkum yavaṉareṉṉum collukkup patil cōṉakar eṉṉum col vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
cōṉār: ivarkaḷ marātti moḻiyil oru piriviṉarākiya koṅkaṇam pēcum taṭṭār. ivarkaḷiṉ cātit talivaṉ mukkiyas taṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ; ivaṉ vaḻakkukaḷi viḷaṅkit tīrppaḷippāṉ. oru kōttirattil ivarkaḷ maṇañ ceytu koḷvatilli. ivarkaḷiṉ paṭṭappeyar ceṭṭi. ceṉṉiyil 408 pērukku oru taṭṭāṉuṇṭu. iṅkilāntil 1100-kku oru taṭṭāṉuṇṭu.
curāṭṭirar: ivarkaḷ paṭṭunūṟkārar eṉappaṭuvārkaḷ. ip peyar ivarkaḷ iruntu vanta curāṭṭira nāṭu toṭarpuṭiya peyar.
takkaṭō: ivarkaḷ maliccātiyiṉarum, pirāmaṇarum kalantu utittōr . ivarkaḷ ceyappūrp pakkaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
taṅkar: marāṭṭiya iṭiyar ip peyar peṟuvar.
taṅkalāṉ: ivarkaḷ paṟiyaril oru piriviṉar. taṅkalāṉ eṉpataṟkuk kiṭṭa niṟkat takātavaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ.
tacārikaḷ: ivarkaḷ oru vaki viṇavap paṇṭāraṅkaḷ. ivarkaḷi oru cātiyiṉar eṉṟu kūṟamuṭiyātu.
taccanāṭaṉ mūppaṉ: itu kuṟiccāṉkaḷukkum nīlakirik kuṟumparkaḷiṟ cilarukkum vaḻaṅkum peyar.
taccaṉ: ivarkaḷ maravēli ceyvōr. paṟiyaril oru piriviṉarum ip peyar peṟuvar.
taṇṭappuliyar: ivarkaḷ teṉ maliyāḷattil vāḻum puliyaril oru piriviṉar. ivarkaḷ taṇṭak koṭiyi aṟuttup piṉṉiya āṭiyi uṭuppar. ip pulik kuṭumpaṅkaḷ illaṅkaḷākak pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. oru illattil uḷḷavarkaḷ atē kuṭumpattil maṇam muṭittal ākātu. ivarkaḷ kuṭiyirukkumiṭam maṉṟam eṉap paṭum. ivarkaḷ cūriyaṉip pārttu “cūriyaṉaṟiya kaṇ keṭṭup pōka” eṉṟu collic cattiyañ ceyvar. ivarkaḷ aiñcu tampurākkaḷ eṉap pāṇṭavari vaḻipaṭuvar; iṟantavarukkuk koḷḷik kuṭam uṭippar; piṇattukku vāykkarici pōṭuvar. iṟantavariṉ āvi cāvār eṉappaṭum.
taṇṭar : maliyāḷattilum , vaḷḷuva nāṭṭilum pālik kāṭṭilum vāḻum īḻavarukku ip peyar vaḻaṅkum. ivarkaḷiṟ peṇkaḷ pala kaṇavari maṇappar. ūrāḷi eṉpatu ivarkaḷiṉ paṭṭappeyar. cila iṭaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ vēḷāṉ eṉavum paṭuvar. cātimāṉkaḷōṭu pēcum pōtu ivarkaḷ tammik kuḻiyar (kuḻiyil vāḻvōr) eṉpar. āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum neṟṟiyil piṟiyum oru puḷḷiyum pacci kuttikkoḷvar. ivarkaḷuḷ kurumār mārttāṇṭa kuruppukkaḷ eṉap paṭuvar. kuruppukkaḷ ampaṭṭaru māvar. ivarkaḷiṉ kaṭavuḷ pattira kāḷi. peṇkaḷ ēḻu allatu eṭṭu vayatākavirukkum pōtu tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam naṭattappaṭum. ik kaliyāṇam kaḻuttuk keṭṭi eṉappaṭum. maṇamakaṉ maccāmpi eṉappaṭuvāṉ. iṟappavarkaḷuḷ kuṭumpattil vayatiṉ mūttavar māttiram iṟantāl cuṭappaṭuvar ivarkaḷukku maraṇat tīṭṭu pattu nāḷ uṇṭu. iṟantavariṉ kiriyikaḷ kaṭaṟkariyil naṭattap paṭum. iṟantavarkaḷukku eḷḷōṭu kalanta uṇavu koṭukkappaṭum. 
taṇṭāṉ: ivarkaḷ tīyar. kirāmattil talimuṟiyāka varum taliyārikaḷ. taliyāri aracaṉāl niyamikkappaṭuvāṉ.
tampalar: teluṅku pēcum kōyiṟ kurumār ip peyar peṟuvar. kōtāvari, kiruṭṭiṇā mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ pirāmaṇarka ḷākavum, teluṅku nāṭṭil cūttirarākavum karutappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ pūṇūlaṇintu koḷvar.
tampi: itu tiruvitāṅkūr nāyarukku vaḻaṅkum mariyātip paṭṭappeyar. tiruvitāṅkūr aracariṉ piḷḷikaḷum tampi eṉap paṭuvar.
tampirāṉ: tiruvāṭutuṟi, mayilam (teṉārkkāṭu) mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kōyiṟ karumaṅkaḷip pārkkum paṇṭāraṅkaḷ tampirāṉ eṉappaṭuvar.
tampurāṉ: tiruvitāṅkūril vāḻum oru kūṭṭattiṉar tampurāṉ eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ irukkum iṭappeyarkaḷāl vēṟupaṭutti aṟiyappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ vaṭa maliyāḷattiluḷḷa kōlāṭṭu nāṭṭi liruntu vantārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ araca kuṭumpattic cērnta peṇkaḷuk kut tālik kaṭṭu allatu paḷḷikkaṭṭuc ceytu avarkaḷōṭu kaṇavar pōla vāḻvar. araca kuṭumpattiluḷḷa āṭavar cūttirap peṇṇik koḷvar. peṇṇiṉ kaṇavaṉ iṟantuviṭṭāl allatu ētum kāraṇatti muṉṉiṭṭup pirintu viṭṭāl avaḷ iṉṉoru kōyiṟṟampurāṉik koḷḷalām. ivarkaḷiṉ kurukkaḷ maliyāḷap pōṟṟikaḷāvar. tiruvitāṅkūr araca kuṭumpattil mutaṟpeṇṇukku ilakkumi eṉṟum, iraṇṭāvatu peṇṇukkup pārvati eṉṟum peyariṭappaṭum.
tarumarācar: ivarkaḷ vaṭa ārkkāṭṭiluḷḷa iruḷaril oru piriviṉar.
taliyāri: ivarkaḷ mutaṉmiyāṉa kirāmak kāvalar. teluṅku nāṭṭil muttirācar kirāmak kāvalarkaḷāvar. avarkaḷ talāri vālu eṉappaṭuvar.
talivar: itu paravariṉ paṭṭappeyar. cātit talivaṉeṉpatu tirunelvēli muttukkuḷikārar talivaṉukku vaḻaṅkum peyar.
tāci: nampūtirip peṇkaḷiṉ pirāmaṇarallāta paṇippeṇ ip peyar peṟuvāḷ. ivaḷ tēvatāciyil vēṟāṉavaḷ.
tāy: itu vaṭa intiyāviṉiṉṟum vanta ciṉariṉ paṭṭap peyar. ivarkaḷ perumpālum vaṇikarāvar.
tārvāṭ: ivarkaḷ marumakkaḷ tāyak kuṭumpattil oru tāy vaṭṭattic cērntōr.
tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam: itu nāyarp peṇkaḷ paruvamaṭiyu muṉ avarkaḷukku naṭattappaṭum kaliyāṇam. marumakkaḷ tāyamuṭiya āṇpeṇ eṉpavarkaḷiṉ oḻukkaṅkaḷ taḷarntavi. ik kaliyāṇam tēvatācikaḷukkuc ceyyappaṭum kaliyāṇam pōṉṟatu.
tikamparar: ivarkaḷ muḻu nirvāṇam paricuttattukku aṭi yāḷameṉak koḷḷum ciṉar.
tirāviṭ: teṉṉintiya pirāmaṇar tirāviṭ eṉappaṭuvar.
tirumuṭi: ceṅkal vēli ceyvōr tirumuṭiyāḷar eṉap paṭuvar. cēlam, kōyamputtūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ perum pālum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷ oḻukkattaḷarvuṭiyar. ivarkaḷ perumpālum vēṭṭuvar allatu kikkōḷarāvar. kōyi lukku nērntu viṭappaṭum kikkōḷap peṇkaḷ kaṭavuḷukkuk kaliyāṇam ceyyappaṭṭavarkaḷākak karutappaṭuvar.
tīpō: ivarkaḷ poṇṭāri vakuppil oru piriviṉar.
tīyaṉ: tīyarum īḻavarum maliyāḷattil kaḷḷiṟakkum cātiyiṉar. vaṭamaliyāḷattil tīyap peṇkaḷukku airōppiyar toṭarpiṉāl piḷḷikaḷ piṟantuḷḷaṉa. ivarkaḷukku marumakkaḷ tāyam uṇṭu. ākavē piḷḷikaḷ tāy vaṭṭattic cērntavarkaḷāvar. muṟkālat til peṇkaḷ airōppiyaruṭaṉ vāḻtal iḻivākak karutappaṭa villi. kalvi aṟivu ēṟappeṟṟa ik kālattil atu iḻivākak karutappaṭukiṟatu. īḻavarum tīyarum ilaṅkiyiliruntu ceṉṟārkaḷ eṉṉum aitīkam uḷḷatu. īḻattu makkaḷ cilar maliyāḷattil vantu kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷeṉṟum avarkaḷ varumpōtu teṉṉiyi (teṟkē uṇṭākum maram) koṇṭu vantārkaḷeṉṟum avarkaḷ tīvār eṉappaṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟum, tīvār eṉpatē tīyār, tīyar eṉṟu āyiṟṟeṉṟum kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nila campantamāṉa āvaṇaṅkaḷil tīyar īḻavar eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. tīyar īḻavarukkut tāḻntavarākak karutappaṭuvar. oruvaṉ cātiyiliruntu taḷḷappaṭṭāl vaṇṇāṉ ampaṭṭaṉ mutaliyōr avaṉukkuk kaṭami ceyya maṟuppārkaḷ. vaṇṇārap peṇkaḷ perumpālum tīya vakuppic cērntavarka ḷāvar. pūppiṉā luṇṭākum tīṭṭu, piḷḷiyip peṟṟa tīṭṭu mutaliyaṉa, nīrāṭiyapiṉ vaṇṇātti koṭukkum “māttu” (māṟṟu) uṭuppatāl nīṅkum. īḻavar māttip paṟṟi aṟiyārkaḷ. tīyaṉ īḻavaṉ camitta uṇavi uṇpāṉ. īḻavaṉ tīyaṉ camitta uṇavi uṇṇa māṭṭāṉ. tīyar kuṭiyirupput tari eṉappaṭum. ovvoru tarikkum ovvoru nāyar atikāriyum. tīyat taṇṭaṉum, cōtiṭaṉum, ampaṭṭa ṉum palavakit toḻilāḷarumiruppar. maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭāriyum maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭāriyum porunta vittu maṇam oḻuṅku ceyvōr caṅkati eṉappaṭuvar. tantiyiṉ kuṭumpam illam eṉavum, tāyiṉ kuṭumpam kulam eṉavum paṭum. orē illattil maṇaṅkaḷ naṭappa tilli. teṉ maliyāḷattil maṇamakaṉ maṟpōrukkuc celkiṉṟa vaṉip pōla ariyil āṭiyik kaṭṭikkoṇṭum, kiyil vāḷ, kēṭakam eṉpavaṟṟip piṭittuk koṇṭum, ivvāṟu uṭuttik koṇṭa iraṇṭu tōḻar pakkattē varak kūttāṭik koṇṭum pōvāṉ. īḻavaṉ oru pōtum vāḷ koṇṭu pōvatilli. kaliyāṇattil mukkiya pakuti vāyil tuṟa pāṭṭu. vaṭamaliyāḷattil ivarkaḷ neṟṟiyilum tōḷilum tirunīṟu pūcikkoḷvar; poṉ kaṭukkaṉ aṇivar. iṭakki mōtira viralil veḷḷi allatu pittaḷi mōtira maṇivar. palar tāyattuk kaṭṭikkoḷvar. muṟkālat til teṉmaliyāḷat tīyar muḻaṅkālukku mēl āṭi uṭuttārkaḷ. ellōrum kātu kuttikkoṇṭaṉar. kātil kaṭukkaṉum ariyil tāyattum aṇintārkaḷ. arikkumēl yātum aṇiyavilli. iṉṟum peṇkaḷ mārpi maṟippatu aritu. īḻavap peṇkaḷ nīla āṭiyip peritumuṭuppar. ippoḻutu ivarkaḷ mārpi maṟit tum āṭiyi aṇikiṟārkaḷ. tīyarp peṇkaḷil cilar aṇiyum tōṭu avarkaḷukkuriyataṉṟu. muṟkālattil pala tīyac cakōtarar kūṭi oru maṉiviyi maṇantu vāḻvārkaḷ. iv vaki nikaḻcciyip paṟṟiya cāṉṟukaḷ eḻuttu mūlam uḷḷaṉa. teṉ maliyāḷattil ivarkaḷukku makkaḷ tāyamuṇṭu. pūppu aṭiyumuṉ peṇṇukkut tālik kaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam naṭipeṟum. iṟantavaṉiṉ tali teṟkē irukkumpaṭi piṇam kiṭattappaṭukiṟatu. kipperuviral kaḷum kālperuviralkaḷum cērttuk kaṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kālmāṭu talimāṭukaḷil viḷakkukaḷ vikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṟantavariṉ uṭal koḷuttappaṭṭāl cāmpal kaṭalil allatu āṟṟil koṭṭappaṭu kiṟatu. āṇṭil oru muṟi iṟantavariṉ niṉivunāḷ koṇṭāṭap paṭukiṉṟatu. appoḻutu vīṭṭiṉ naṭuvē viḷakkuk koḷutti vittup pakkattē taṇṇīrum iḷanīrum vikkappaṭum. ivarkaḷ māṭṭu iṟicciyi uṇpatilli. 
turuvāḷar: ivarkaḷ kaṭṭuk koṭukkiṟa (kaliyāṇam porutti vikkiṟa) cātiyiṉar. ivarkaḷukku vēṇṭāṉ eṉpatum maṟupeyar.
tulāpāram: itu tiruvitāṅkūr aracarāl ceyyappaṭum oru vakit tāṉam. aracaṉ taṉatu niṟiyuḷḷa poṉṉip pirāmaṇarukkut tāṉañ ceyvāṉ.
tulukkar (turukkiyar) : ip peyar cila camayaṅkaḷil makamatiyarik kuṟikka vaḻaṅkappaṭum
tēcikar: ivarkaḷ paṇṭāraṅkaḷuḷ oru piriviṉar.
tēcāri: vaṭa ārkkāṭṭil ovvoru iṭattilumuḷḷa taliyāri tēcāri eṉappaṭuvaṉ.
tēvaṭiyāḷ: tēvatāci
tēvatāci: tācikaḷil ēḻu vakiyiṉarkaḷuṇṭu. (1) tattam - taṉṉik kōyilukku oppaṭittavaḷ. (2) vikkirakam - taṉṉik kōyilukku viṟṟavaḷ (3) pirittiya - taṉ kuṭumpa naṉmikkāka kōyiṟ paṇiviṭi ceypavaḷ. (4) patti kāraṇamākak kōyili aṭipavaḷ (5) tāṉē virumpi vantu cērpavaḷ (6) alaṅkārañ ceytu aracarāl kōyilukkuk koṭukkappaṭṭavaḷ. ivarkaḷiṉ vēli vikkiraṅkaḷuk kuc cāmari vīcutal, kumpa ārātti koṇṭu cellutal, kaṭavuḷ ulāvarum pōtu āṭippāṭutal eṉpaṉa. 1004il irācarācaṉ kaṭṭiya periya tañcāvūrk kōyiliṉ nāṉku vītikaḷilum nāṉūṟu taḻukkuc cērippeṇkaḷ vāḻntārkaḷ. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟuti yil kāñcīpurattil nūṟu āṭal mātar iruntārkaḷ. maturi, kāñcī puram mutaliya kōyilkaḷil iṉṉum perun tokiyiṉar kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷukkuk kōyil varuvāyi liruntu māṉiyaṅ kiṭikkiṟatu. aptūr iracāk (Abdul Rasak) eṉṉum turukkiya tūtar 15ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vicayanakarattiliruntār. ip peṇkaḷ mūlam kiṭikkum varuvāy nakarak kāvalar (Police) campaḷam mutaliyavaṟṟukkuc ceṉṟateṉa avar kūṟiyuḷḷār. ip peṇkaḷukkum vāḷukkum allatu kaṭavuḷukkum kōyilil kaliyāṇam naṭattappaṭukiṉṟatu. peṇkaḷiṉ kaḻuttil avarkaḷ kulattiluḷḷa āṭavar tāli kaṭṭu kiṉṟaṉar. tāci kulattavarkaḷukku āṇkaḷip pōlavē peṇkaḷuk kum cotturimi uṇṭu. tāci kula āṭavar naṭṭuvar eṉappaṭuvar. avarkaḷ tamatu peyaruṭaṉ piḷḷi eṉpatic cērttu vaḻaṅkuvar. tācikaḷil valaṅki, iṭaṅki eṉa iru pirivukaḷuṇṭu. valaṅkiyiṉar iṭaṅkik kammāḷar vīṭukaḷil pāṭavō āṭavō māṭṭārkaḷ. maṟṟavarkaḷ kammāḷat tācikaḷ eṉappaṭuvārkaḷ. teluṅku nāṭṭil kammāḷar pōkam allatu cāṇi eṉappaṭuvar. oriya nāṭṭut tācikaḷ kuṉi eṉappaṭuvar. pellāriyiṉ mēṟkut tālukkāvilum ataṉ ayalēyuḷḷa tārvar, micūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum pōyar, piṉā cūs mutaliyavarkaḷuḷ āṇ kuḻanti illāta kuṭumpaṅkaḷuḷ oru peṇ kuḻanti tāciyākak kōyilukku viṭappaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu caṭṭamuṇṭu. tiruvitāṅkūrt tācikaḷ tēvaṭiyāṭkaḷ, kuṭikkāri (vīṭṭuk kuriyavaḷ) allatu peṇkaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. āṭavar tēvaṭi yāṉ allatu nāñcil nāṭṭu vēḷāḷaṉ eṉappaṭuvar. tiruvitāṅ kūrt tācikaḷ vīṭukaḷil cēvikkap pōvatilli. tēvaṭiyāḷ mutumi yaṭintuviṭṭāl tōṭu kaḷiyum caṭaṅku naṭattappaṭukiṟatu. itu aracaṉatu araṇmaṉiyil naṭakkum. piṉpu avaḷ tāykkiḻavi ākiviṭu vāḷ. kēraḷattil muṟakkuṭi ciṟappukkuṭi eṉa iruvakit tācikaḷ uḷar. muṟakkuṭip peṇkaḷ mukkiya kālaṅkaḷil māttiram cēvippar. tiruvitāṅkūrt tācikaḷiṉ cotturimi peṇkaḷic cārkiṉṟatu.
tēvar: putuccēriyil kaṇṇāṭip poruḷkaḷil vāṇikañ ceyyum teluṅkar tēvar eṉappaṭuvar. kirāma tēvatikaḷukkup pūci ceyyum pūcārikaḷum tēvar eṉappaṭuvar. maṟavariṉ paṭṭappeyar tēvaṉ. teṉ kaṉṉaṭattiṟ kaḷḷiṟakkuvōr tēvaṟukaḷu (tēvariṉ makkaḷ) eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ ilaṅkiyiliruntu vantavarkaḷākak karutappaṭuvar.
tēvāṅkar: ceṉṉi mākāṇam muḻumiyilum kāṇappaṭum tuḷuvum kaṉṉaṭamum pēcum necavut toḻilāḷar ip peyar peṟuvar. kōyamputtūrp pakutiyil ivarkaḷ ceṭṭukkāraṉ eṉappaṭu vārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ cuvēsvari eṉṉum teyvatti vaḻipaṭuvar. iv vaḻipāṭu kāḷi, turkki vaḻipāṭṭiṉ vēṟupāṭākum. ivarkaḷ peritum civa matattiṉar; iliṅkaṅkaṭṭik koḷvar. nāṭṭumakkaḷ pala erutukaḷi vaḷarppar. erutu iṟantuviṭṭāl tukkam koṇ ṭāṭip palar piṉtoṭarntu cella ataṉi eṭuttuc ceṉṟu putippar.
tēvātikkar: ivarkaḷ kaṉṉaṭattil kōyiṟṟoṇṭu ceyyum kaṉṉaṭam pēcum makkaḷ. mayūravarmaṉ, pūci māttiram pirāmaṇar ceytal vēṇṭumeṉṟum, maṟṟa vēlikaḷ stāṉikar, tēvātik karkaḷ ceyya vēṇṭumeṉṟum vitittāṉ. ippoḻutu ivarkaḷiṟ palar uḻavuttoḻil ceykiṉṟaṉar. peṇkaḷiṉ cotturimi peṇ kaḷic cārvatu.
toṇṭamāṉ: ivarkaḷ tirunelvēlip pakutiyil vāḻum cuṇṇāmpukkārar. ivarkaḷ putukkōṭṭiyiliruntu tirunelvēli yiṟ ceṉṟu kuṭiyēṟiya kaḷḷaril oru piriviṉar. ivarkaḷil toṇṭa māṉ, tōlmēstiri eṉa iru piriviṉaruṇṭu.
tommārar: ivarkaḷ ōriṭattil taṅkātu tiriyum kaḻik kūttar. ivarkaḷ teluṅku marāṭṭiyar; intustāṉi pēcuvar. ivarkaḷ cātippaṟiyarilum uyarntavar. āriyak kūttāṭikaḷ eṉpatum ivarkaḷukkup peyar. kaḻik kūttut toṭaṅkumuṉ ivarkaḷ cāṇiyi ṉāl piḷḷiyār piṭittuvittu ataṉi vaḻipaṭuvar.
tōṭar( tōtar) : ivarkaḷ nīlakiri maliyil vāḻvar; erumi kaḷi vaḷarppar; tamiḻ pēcuvar. ivarkaḷukkuc curuṇṭu toṅkum taṭitta talimayirum aṭarnta tāṭiyum uṇṭu. peṇkaḷ pūppaṭinta piṉ pacci kuttikkoḷvar. peṇkaḷ māṭṭut toḻuvattil eriyum koḷḷik kaṭṭiyit toṭṭāl tīṭṭu uṇṭākum. tōtariṉ toḻuvattukkup pirāmaṇaṉ cellutal ākātu. tōtar perum pālum mōril cōṟu ākkuvar. peṇkaḷ pala kaṇavari maṇappar. tōtappeṇ orutti oruvaṉukku maṉiviyākumpōtu avaḷ avaṉ cakōtarar ellārukkum maṉivi eṉpatu viḷaṅkik koḷḷattakkatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil kaṇavar cakōtararāyirāviṭṭālum orē kūṭṭat tic cērntavarāyiruppar. iraṇṭu allatu pala cakōtarar pala peṇkaḷit tamakkup potumaṉiviyarāka vittiruppar. iṟanta variṉ uṭal cuṭappaṭum. appoḻutu cuṭaliyil erumi paliyiṭap paṭum.
tōṭṭi: ivarkaḷ cakkiliyaruḷ oru piriviṉar. tañcāvūrt tōṭṭiyāṉ allatu kampaḷattāṉ paṉṟivaḷarttal , pāmpu piṭittal, picciyeṭuttal mutaliya toḻilkaḷic ceyvāṉ. tōṭṭi tanti yiṉ cakōtari makaḷi allatu tāymāmaṉ makaḷik kaliyāṇam ceyya lām. ik kaṭṭuppāṭu iruppatāl ciṟuvarukku vaḷarnta peṇkaḷ kaliyāṇam muṭikkappaṭuvar. appoḻutu maṇamakaṉiṉ tanti peṇ ṇōṭu cērntu piḷḷikaḷip peṟuvāṉ. tōṭṭiyāṉ pirāmaṇaṉ vīṭṭil uṇṇamāṭṭāṉ. itaṟkuk kāraṇam pulappaṭavilli. peṇkaḷ kuṭumpa vaṭṭattukkuḷ puriyum viyapicāram kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭamāṭṭātu. tōṭṭikaḷiṉ kuṭittalivaṉ ūr nāyakaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. maṇamāṉa peṇkaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ uṟaviṉarukkut tamatu tayavik koṭuttal kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭamāṭṭātu. vāyilil oruvaṉiṉ ceruppu iruppatik kaṇṭāl kaṇavaṉ uḷḷē cella māṭṭāṉ. ivarkaḷ iṟantavarik kal naṭṭu vaḻipaṭuvar.
tōṇi: tōṇi pēḻi pōṉṟatu. 70 aṭi nīḷamum 20 aṭi akalamum 11 aṭi āḻamumuṭiyatu. aṭi taṭṭiyāka virukkum. aṭippakuti 7 aṭi akalamuṭiyatu. teṉṉintiyāvil aṭikkappaṭṭa īya, cempu nāṇayaṅkaḷil tōṇi poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 
ṭōpi(Dhobi): itu vaṇṇāṉukku intiyā muḻuvatilum vaḻaṅkum peyar. itu kaḻuvu eṉṉum poruḷ taruvatu. tāva eṉṉum camakkiruta aṭiyākap piṟantatu. ivarkaḷ ātiyil oricā mākāṇatti liruntu vantārkaḷ. maṇak kālaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ ēḻu vīṭukaḷiliruntu ēḻu kuṭattil taṇṇīr koṇṭuvantu maṇamakaṉiyum peṇṇiyum muḻukkāṭṭuvārkaḷ. maṇakkiriyiyil iruvariṉ iṭakkiyum cērttuk kaṭṭappaṭum. pantaliliruppōr iruvar mītum mañcaḷiyum ariciyiyum eṟivārkaḷ.
tōriyar: ivarkaḷ kōyamputtūr, cēlam māvaṭṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ muṟkālattil mīṉ piṭippōrum pallakkuc cumappōrumāka iruntaṉar. ippoḻutu ivarkaḷ veṟṟili payiriṭu var. ivarkaḷiṉ cātittalivaṉ ecamāṉaṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ. ivaṉ ivarkaḷiṭiyē uḷḷa vaḻakkukaḷi viḷaṅkit tīrppaṉ.
nakarattār: ivarkaḷ ceṭṭikaḷil oru piriviṉar. nakarameṉpatu paṭṭiṉam. ivarkaḷ muṉpu kāñcīpurattiliruntārkaḷeṉpatu aitīkam. ivarkaḷiṉ vēliyāḷ cātippiḷḷi eṉappaṭuvaṉ. vitavikaḷ maṟumaṇañ ceyvatilli.
naṅkuṭi vēḷāḷaṉ: ivarkaḷ tirunelvēliyiṟ pala pākaṅ kaḷilē vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. kōṭṭi vēḷāḷarirukkum ciṟīvikuntak kōṭṭikku naṅkuṭi vēḷāḷap peṇkaḷ pōka aṉumatikkappaṭuva tilli. ivarkaḷuḷ oruvar iṟantupōṉāl ac ceytiyi ampaṭṭaṉ aṟivippāṉ. akkiṉi eṉṉum makāmuṉi tavamceytu koṇṭirukkum pōtu teyvap peṇkaḷ nīrāṭavantārkaḷeṉṟum muṉivar avarkaḷik kātalittu mūṉṟu kumārarip peṟṟār eṉṟum avarkaḷi vēḷāḷarkaḷ vaḷarttārka ḷeṉṟum avarkaḷē cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya aracarkaḷ eṉṟum aitīkaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
nampiṭi: ivarkaḷ maliyāḷattil kāṇappaṭum nampūtirikaḷ pōṉṟa oru kūṭṭattiṉar. ivarkaḷ pūṇūlaṇivar. ivarkaḷukku marumakkaḷ tāyamuṇṭu. tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam ivarkaḷ contac cātiyiṉarāl ceyyappaṭum. nampūtirimārum, ic cāti āṭavarum nampiṭip peṇkaḷic campantaṅ koḷvar. nampiṭi āṭavar nāyarp peṇkaḷic campantam vikkalām. ivarkaḷ viruntukaḷil nampūtirika ḷōṭu iruntu uṇpar. peṇkaḷ maṉōlapāṭu eṉappaṭuvar.
nampūtiri: kēraḷa uṟpatti eṉṉum nūl maliyāḷa moḻiyi luḷḷatu. atu paracurāmar kaṉṉiyākumariyiliruntu varuṇaṉi nōkkit tavañ ceytu kaṉṉiyākumari mutal kōkarṇam variyiluḷḷa nilattip peṟṟāreṉṟum, paracurāmarāṟ kuṭiyēṟṟappaṭṭavarkaḷē nampūtirikaḷ eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivarkaḷiṉ urimi vaḻi marumakkaḷ tāyam. nampūtirip peṇkaḷ veḷiyē cellumpōtu tammi vikkāṉ kuṭiyāl maṟittukkoḷvar. viricāli eṉṉum nāyarp peṇ avarkaḷiṉ muṉṉē celvāḷ. nampūtirip peṇkaḷ veḷiyē cellumpōtu tammik kaḻuttu mutal kālvariyum tuṇiyāl pōrttu maṟittukkoḷvar. ivarkaḷ naki aṇital kūṭātu. ivarkaḷip paracurāmar koṇṭu vantu kuṭiyēṟṟiṉār eṉṉum kati nampattakkataṉṟu. ivarkaḷ ki.pi. 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum 8ām nūṟṟāṇṭukkum iṭiyil maliyāḷattil kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷākalām. tālami, peripuḷūs eṉpōr pirāmaṇar maliyāḷattiṟ kuṭiyēṟi yiruntamiyi ki.pi. mutalām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. mēṟkuc cāḷukkiya aracar valimi peṟṟirunta ki.pi. 4ām, 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil iṅku vantu taṅkiya pirāmaṇar nampūtirikaḷōṭu kalantu oṉṟupaṭṭiruttal kūṭum. cāḷukkiyarukkup paṉṟik koṭi uṇṭu. ātaliṉ cāḷukkiyap pirāmaṇar kuṭiyēṟiya iṭam paṉṉiyūr (paṉṟiyūr) eṉappeyar peṟṟirukkalām. cēvūrp (civaṉūr) pirāmaṇar civa camayattiṉarāvar. ivarkaḷ cāḷukkiyarukkup piṉ vanta cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya aracarkaḷiṉ camayattiyē piṉpaṟṟiṉārkaḷākalām. perumāḷeṉṉumaracar cēranāṭṭukku ayalē iruntu vantavarkaḷāka lām. ivarkaḷ tamatu conta nāṭṭi viṭṭuvantamiyāl ivarkaḷ nampūtirip peṇkaḷi maṇantukoḷḷa nampūtirikaḷ maṟṟa vakuppup peṇkaḷic campantaṅ koṇṭārkaḷākalām. ākavē marumakkaḷ tāyam avaciyamāyiṟṟu. kēraḷattiṉ cila pakutikaḷ atikārikaḷāl āḷappaṭṭatu. vaḷḷuva nāṭallāta maṟṟiya iṭaṅkaḷil nampūtirikaḷ atikārikaḷāka viruntirukkiṉṟaṉar.
nampūtirimār kōyiṟ pūji ceyvar. ivarkaḷ aracariṉ tūta rākavum celvar. maliyāḷattil paṭṭarp pirāmaṇar aṅkumiṅkum alintu tirivar. avarkaḷukku uṇavu nampūtirikaḷ illaṅkaḷilum kōvilakaṅkaḷilum kōvilkaḷilum kiṭikkum. nampūtirikaḷukku ēṭu (vētam) paṭittalum paṭippittalum, picci, ōtu, aṭukkaḷi katavu (ellāp pirāmaṇarum kuḷikkumiṭattil niṉṟu kuḷittal) āṭu (vēḷvi) canti, kuruvāyiruntu kōyiṟ kiriyi ceytal, araṅku, panti (ellāp pirāmaṇarōṭu miruntu uṇṇutal) pōṉṟa urimikaḷ uṇṭu. ivarkaḷ talimayiri neṟṟikku muṉṉāl toṅkumpaṭi muṭintuviṭuvar. nallanāḷ pārttu mukattiyum taliyiyum maḻittuk koḷvar. maṉivi karuppavatiyāṉāl nampūtiri mayiri vaḷaraviṭuvāṉ. ivarkaḷukku aṭarnta mayiruṇṭu. ivarkaḷiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷuṭ cila tōtariṭiyē kāṇappaṭuvaṉa pōlvaṉa. ataṉāl ivarkaḷ tōtaril oru piriviṉar eṉṟu karutappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ viral nakaṅkaḷi nīḷamāka vaḷaraviṭuvar. āṭavariṉ uṭukkum vaki “taṭṭut tūkkuka” eṉappaṭum. nampūtirikaḷ miritaṭi tarippar. peṇkaḷ uṭukkum vaki “okkum koluttum vaccut tūkkuka” eṉappaṭum. āṭavar kātil tuḷiyiṭṭuk kuṇṭalamaṇivar. peṇkaḷ cūṭṭu eṉṉum kātaṇi ceruttāli mutaliyavaṟṟi aṇivar. avarkaḷ mūkkit tuḷiyiṭṭu aṇiyum aṇikaḷi aṇivatilli; nīrāṭiyapiṉ neṟṟiyil cantaṉat tāl mūṉṟu kuṟikaḷ vippar. atu ampulik kuṟi eṉappaṭum. avarkaḷ oru pōtum kuṅkuma maṇivatilli; mañcaḷ kuḷippatu milli. āṭavar ki viralil pavittiram eṉṉum āḻi aṇivārkaḷ; pūṇūlil yantirat takaṭu aṭitta kūṭṭit toṅkaviṭuvārkaḷ. paṉṉiraṇṭu vayatup piyaṉ kavarimāṉ tōl vāri iṭatu tōḷukku mēlāl pōṭṭiruppāṉ. iraṇṭu talippukaḷum at tōliṉālēyē poruttit tikkappaṭṭirukkum. ivarkaḷ tirumaṇattukkup piṉ pūṇūli māṟṟik koḷvatilli. teṉṉintiya pirāmaṇar ōr āṇṭukku oru muṟi pūṇūli māṟṟuvar. nampūtiri irukkum vaḷavuk kup peyaruṇṭu. vīṭṭiṉ vaṭakiḻakkil toḻuvam irukkum. atu kōcāli eṉappaṭum. ivarkaḷ iruppataṟkup perumpālum māṉ tōlip payaṉpaṭuttuvar. nampūtiriyiṉ mūtta kumāraṉ kāraṇavaṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ. avaṉ uṇavi uṇṭa piṉ eḻumpōtu iṭakkiyāl iliyit toṭuvāṉ; maṉivi valakkiyāl toṭuvāḷ. appo ḻutu atu eccil ākamāṭṭātu. avaḷ av viliyil uṇavip pucippāḷ. nampūtiri, paṭṭar camitta uṇavi uṇṇalām. curikkāy, paṉampaḻam, paṉaṅkaṭṭi mutaliyavikaḷi nampūtiri uṇpatilli. pāl pāyacamāka māttiram (piratamaṉ) uṇṇappaṭum. nampūtiri mantira vātikaḷ oruvaṉik kollavēṇṭumāṉāl avaṉip pōṉṟa vaṭivi ulōkat takaṭṭil eḻuti ataṉ kīḻ yantiram varintu mantiram cepippārkaḷ. piṉpu ataṉi iṉṉoru ulōkat takaṭṭil vittuc curuṭṭi ati avaṉ kuṭiyirukku miṭattukkuk kiṭṭap putippārkaḷ. avaṉ av viṭattil mitittāl uṭaṉē mantiram palita mākum. avaṉ iṟantu viṭuvāṉ. allatu ētum tuyarattukku āḷā vāṉ. mantira vittikkārar kuṭṭiccāttāṉi vālāyam ceytu koḷvārkaḷ eṉṟu maliyāḷattil nampappaṭukiṟatu. āṇ kuḻanti yillāta nampūtiri maṉiviyiṉ maraṇattukkup piṉ mayiri ōrāṇṭukku vaḷaraviṭuvāṉ. tāy allatu tantikkuk kaṭami ceyyum mūtta makaṉum avvāṟē ōrāṇṭukku mayiri nīḷa vaḷaraviṭuvāṉ. mayiri vaḷaraviṭum kālattil muruṅkikkāy, pāl, miḷakāy, tuvari, avari, pappaṭam mutaliya uṇavukaḷ nīkkappaṭum. kuḷikkumpōtellāmal ivarkaḷ kupīṉa maṇivatilli. kaṭicip perumāḷ arāpiyāvuk kuc ceṉṟa kālam mutal (ki.pi.825) maliyāḷa āṇṭu kaṇakkiṭappaṭum. ovvoru mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷukku oru muṟi ‘atika’ eṉṉum oru nāḷ cērkkappaṭum.
nāyar nampūtirikku āṟu aṭikkuḷḷum, ampaṭṭaṉ 12 aṭik kuḷḷum, tīyaṉ 36 aṭikkuḷḷum, maliyaṉ 64 aṭikkuḷḷum, puliyaṉ 96 aṭikkuḷḷum varutal kūṭātu. uyarnta cātiyāṉ vīti vaḻiyē cellum pōtu tāḻnta cātiyāṉ vilakic cellumpaṭi cattamiṭu vāṉ. tāḻnta cātiyāṉ pōkum pōtutāṉ celvati uyarnta cātiyārukku aṟivittaṟkum, uyarnta cātiyāriṉ cattattik kēṭṭut tāṉ vilaki niṟṟaṟkum cattamiṭuvāṉ. ivarkaḷ orē kōttirattil maṇappatilli. maṇamāṉa peṇ kaṇavaṉiṉ kōttirattic cērntu viṭukiṟāḷ. maṇamākātu iṟanta peṇkaḷukku maṇakkiriyi ceytu tāli kaṭṭappaṭum. tōtar cātiyiṉarum ivvāṟē ceyvar. maṇattiṉ pōtu peṇvīṭṭār maṇamakaṉ vīṭṭukkuc ceṉṟu avaṉi aḻittu varuvārkaḷ. maṇamakaṉ vikkāṉ kuṭiyiṉ kīḻc celvāṉ. nāyarp peṇkaḷum nāyarum nampūtirikaḷum vāyilil niṉṟu pavaṉi varuvārkaḷ. maṇamakaṉ tuṭṭa tēvatikaḷ aṇukātapaṭi kiyil kaṅkaṇaṅ kaṭṭik koṇṭum, patiṉāṟu kaṇuvuḷḷa mūṅkil, mukam pārkkum kaṇṇāṭi, ampu, nālu cēli, tāli mutaliyavaṟṟik koṇṭum varuvāṉ. vāyilil nampūtirip peṇkaḷip pōl uṭutta nāyarp peṇkaḷ pavaṉi varupavarkaḷi etirkoḷvārkaḷ; maṇamaka ṉiṉ mukattukketirāka ālatti kāṭṭuvārkaḷ. caṭaṅkiṉ muṉ pakuti “nanti mukam” (puṇṇiya āka vacaṉam) eṉappaṭum. maṇamakaḷi maṇa vaṟikkuk koṇṭu celva taṉ muṉ maṇamakaḷiṉ tanti maṇamakaṉiṉ kālik kaḻuvuvāṉ. nāyarp peṇkaḷ āyirantiri eṉṉum ālat tiyik kāṭṭuvar. maṇappantal tūṇkaḷukkuc civappu āṭi cuṟṟap paṭṭirukkum. maṇamakaṉiyum maṇamakaḷiyum maṇavaṟikku aḻit tuc cellumpōtu oruvari oruvar pārātapaṭi naṭuvil vikkāṉ kuṭi piṭikkappaṭum. avarkaḷ oruvari oruvar pārkkuñ caṭaṅku muka taricaṉam eṉappaṭum. maṇamakaḷum maṇamakaṉum mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷ pirintu taṉiyē iruppar. nālām nāḷ iruvariyum paṭukkiyaṟikkuc koṇṭu ceṉṟu aṟiyiṉ katavit tāḻiṭṭuviṭṭu kurukkaḷ veḷiyē niṉṟu culōkañ colluvar. aintāvatu nāḷ kuḷattil nīrāṭiyapiṉ iruvarum mīṉ piṭippārkaḷ. mīṉ piṭikkum kiriyi, muṉpu mīṉ piṭippavarkaḷākaviruntavarkaḷip paracurāmar pirāmaṇarākkiṉār eṉṉum aitīkattik kuṟikkiṉṟateṉpar. nampūtirikaḷil viyapicāra naṭattiyuḷḷa peṇkaḷ kaṭumiyākat taṇ ṭikkappaṭuvārkaḷ. viyapicāri eṉṟu cantēkikkappaṭṭavaḷ aiñcām puram allatu pañcōlippuram eṉṉum iṭattil viṭappaṭuvāḷ. appoḻutu akakkōyiṉmār, puṟakkōyiṉmār eṉpōr vantirup pārkaḷ. akakkōyiṉmār oḻukkattip pātukāppōr. puṟakkōyiṉ mār aracaṉ cārpil vantiruppōr. vicāraṇit talivaṉ smārt taṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ. smārttaṉāl ceyyappaṭum vicāraṇi smārtta vicāraṇi eṉappaṭum. kuṟṟaccāṭṭu uṟutiyāṉāl peṇṇiṉ kiṭṭiya uṟaviṉaṉ avaḷukkuc ceyya vēṇṭiya antiyak kiriyi kaḷic ceyvāṉ.
camayacampantamākap patiṉeṭṭuc caṅkaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. avaṟṟiṉ talivaṉ vākkiya virutti eṉappaṭuvāṉ. avaṉ ōttu nampūtiriyāka viruttal vēṇṭum. avaṉukku aṭuttapaṭiyiluḷḷavaṉ pāskaraṉ. ivaṉ cāstirikaḷukku (attirap payiṟcikku)k kuru. katikaḷi eṉpatu maliyāḷattu nāṭakam. ivarkaḷ acayākam eṉṉum āṭṭukkaṭā yākam ceytu yākattil koṉṟa āṭṭiṉ iṟicciyi uṇpar. ciṟuvar pūṇūlaṇivar. muṟkālap peṇkaḷum pūṇūlaṇintārkaḷ. pūṇūl kiruṣṇamirukā eṉṉum māṉ tōliṉāl iṭappaṭum.
narikkāl: nari aṅkammāviṉ vākaṉam. ip peyar narikkompu ceyyum kuruvikkāraṉukkum vaḻaṅkum.
nākar: kañcam, vicākappaṭṭiṉap pakkaṅkaḷil vāḻum palar tamatu kulam nāka vamicameṉpar. kurṉiyar, toriyar eṉpōr tirumaṇak kālattil pāmpukaḷuṟiyum eṟumpup puṟṟukaḷi vaṇaṅkuvar. paḷḷikaḷil oru piriviṉar nākapaṭam eṉṉum pāmpiṉ talipōṉṟa oru vaki aṇiyi aṇivar. avarkaḷ nākar eṉappaṭuvar.
nākavāculu: vicākappaṭṭiṉap pakkaṅkaḷil uḻavuttoḻil ceyyum makkaḷ ip peyar peṟuvar. vivākatti virumpātirukkum makaḷir viyapicārattāl poruḷīṭṭuvar; vīṭukaḷil naṭaṉamāṭuvar. ivarkaḷ nāyuṭu cātikkut tāḻntōr.
nāñcil nāṭṭu vēḷāḷar: nāñcil nāṭṭu vēḷāḷar eṉṉum oru piriviṉar tiruvitāṅkūr muḻumiyilum aṅkumiṅkum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ mukkiya iṭam tōvalā (Tovala). ivarkaḷiṉ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷ vēḷāḷar paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷil vēṟāṉavi. ivarkaḷ taṅkaḷ peyarkaḷuṭaṉ kaṇakkuppiḷḷi eṉṉum paṭṭap peyaric cērttuk koḷvar. ki.pi. 825il cīriya kiṟittavarukku aḷikkappaṭṭa naṉkoṭip paṭṭiyattil oru tacca kuṭumpamum nāṉku vēḷāḷak kuṭumpaṅkaḷum kaṭaṟkariyil maraṅkaḷi vittu uṇṭākkutaṟkup poṟuppuṭiyaṉa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. vēḷāḷar kārāḷar eṉappaṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. pāṇṭiya aracar in nāṭṭiṉ cila pakutikaḷi āṇṭa kālattu iv vēḷāḷar vantu kuṭi yēṟiyiruttal kūṭum. palar nāñcil nāṭṭu vēḷāḷar eṉṉum peyari viṭṭu nāyar eṉṉum peyari vittukkoṇṭaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ mutaṉmiyuṭiya teyvaṅkaḷ māṭaṉ, icakki, iṉaṉ mutaliyaṉa. nāñcil nāṭṭu vēḷāḷariṉ kiriyi campantamākap pāṭappaṭum pāṭṭu villaṭiccāṉ pāṭṭu eṉappaṭum. avarkaḷiṉ viḻākkaḷiṟ ciṟantatu ammaṉkoṭi eṉpatu. cittiri mātattu amāvāciyaṉṟu cittira puttiraṉ kati kōyilkaḷil paṭikkappaṭum. ivarkaḷiṟ palar ‘kāriyas taṉ’ mutalpiṭi (pokkiṣakkāraṉ) kaṇakkaṉ mutaliya aluvalkaḷil amarntuḷḷārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ paruvamaṭintapiṉ maṇappar. tāy māmaṉ makaḷ. ciṟiyatāy makaḷ ciṟanta maṇamakaḷirākak koḷḷappaṭuvar. maṇamakaṉukkuk koṭukkum paricukaḷil muṇṭu, irumpu eḻuttāṇi, katti mutaliyaṉa aṭaṅkum. nāñcil nāṭṭu vēḷāḷar nāyarp peṇ kaḷiṭiyē campantaṅ koḷvar. kaṇavaṉ viṭumuṟi eḻutik koṭuttuk kaliyāṇa nīkkam ceytu koḷḷalām. āṇ piḷḷikaḷukkut tanti tēṭiya cottil nāliloṉṟum, tanti vaḻi varum cottila nāli loṉṟum cērum. atu ukantutāmā eṉappaṭum. kaliyāṇam taḷḷap paṭṭa peṇṇukkuk kaṇavaṉiṭam cīviya kālam variyum vāḻkkic celavu peṟa urimiyuṇṭu. itaṟkāka avaḷ urimi kōrum cottu naṅku (ki) tāmā eṉappaṭum. (naṅku) - peṇ.
nāṭāṉ: cāṇāṉ.
nāṭṭuk kōṭṭic ceṭṭi: nāṭṭukkōṭṭic ceṭṭimār peritum vaṭṭikkup paṇaṅ koṭuttu vāṅkuvar. iv vakiyil ivarkaḷ teṉṉintiya yūtarkaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ tattup piḷḷi mañcaḷ nīrp piḷḷi eṉappaṭuvar. ceṭṭikaḷ talimayiri maḻittuk koḷvar. ippoḻutu iv vaḻakkam perumpālum niṉṟu pōyviṭṭatu. peṇkaḷum, āṇkaḷum kātit tuḷiyiṭṭukkoḷ var. ciṟumiyar maṇikaḷum cippikaḷum kōtta mālikaḷi aṇivar. ivarkaḷukkuk kōyil vāyil maṟiyal, maṭattuvāyil maṟiyal eṉa iraṇṭu pañcāyattukkaḷuṇṭu. piṇaccaṭaṅkukku iṭumpantal koṭ ṭiṟ pantal eṉappaṭum. maṇattukku iṭumpantal koṭṭaki allatu kāvaṉam eṉappaṭum.
nāyak: nāykā - talivaṉ. ivarkaḷ vicayanakara aracar cila pirāmaṇak kuṭumpaṅkaḷukkum ip peyaruṇṭu. muṟkālattil ceṉṉi nakaril ‘pōlis’ mēl atikāri vēli petta nāyakkar kaḷukkuk koṭukkappaṭṭatu. teṉ kaṉṉaṭattil tēvaṭiyāḷ nāykaṉi eṉappaṭuvāḷ.
nāyar: nāyar eṉpōr maliyāḷattil kāṇappaṭum oru cātiyiṉar. muṟkāla nāyar uḻavuttoḻil ceypavarkaḷākavum, pōr vīrarkaḷākavum iruntārkaḷ. ippoḻutu vaṇikar, kittoḻilkaḷ purivōr, eṇṇey vāṇikar, pallakkuc cumappōr, ampaṭṭar, vaṇṇār mutaliya pala piriviṉar nāyar vakuppiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu vantu maliyāḷattiṟ kuṭiyēṟiya makkaḷ palar nāyariṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷip piṉpaṟṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. cila kālaṅkaḷil aracar tāḻnta vakuppiṉar cilari nāyarkaḷākkiyuḷḷār kaḷ. ki.pi. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭil makamatiya pirayāṇi oruvaṉ eḻutiyirup patu varumāṟu: aracaṉ paṭṭattukku varumuṉ oruvakik kiriyi cey yappaṭukiṉṟatu. aracaṉukku etiril ōr aḷavu cōṟu paṭikkappaṭukiṉ ṟatu. nāṉūṟu allatu ainnūṟu pēr vantu aracaṉuṭiya kiyiliruntu ciṟitu cōṟu vāṅki uṇkiṟārkaḷ. tāṉ mutaliṟ cōṟṟi uṇṭapiṉ maṟṟavarkaḷukkuk koṭukkiṟāṉ. aracaṉ iṟakkum pōtu allatu kollappaṭṭa pōtu tām uṭaṉkaṭṭi ēṟuvatāka avarkaḷ cattiyañ ceykiṟārkaḷ. pūrccās (Purchas) eṉpavaṉ eḻutiyiruppatu varumāṟu: kocci yaracaṉiṭam palar irukkiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷ amocci eṉap paṭuvar. cilar nāyar eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ tamatu aracaṉukkāka uyiri viṭa āyattamākavirukkiṟārkaḷ. maliyāḷa moḻiyil ivarkaḷ cāvār eṉappaṭuvārkaḷ. kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉiṉ kalveṭṭil (ki.pi. 1083) avaṉ kuṭanāṭṭi veṉṟapōtu nāyariṉ muṉṉōrākiya vīrar iṟuti variyil pōr ceytu maṭintārkaḷ eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 16m nūṟṟāṇṭil pārpōcā (Barbosa) eṉpavar eḻutiyiruppatu varumāṟu: ivarkaḷukkup pōr ceyvatiyaṉṟi vēṟu vēliyilli. ivarkaḷ eppoḻutum vāḷ, vil, ampu, kēṭayam, īṭṭi mutaliyavi kaḷik koṇṭu celvar. ivarkaḷil cilar aracaruṭaṉum cilar aracaṉiṉ uṟaviṉarākiya pirapukkaḷōṭu miruppārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ ēḻu vayatilēyē āyutap payiṟci peṟuvar. 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil hamilṭaṉ eḻutiyiruppatu varumāṟu: camariṉ eṉṉum maliyāḷa aracaṉ 12 āṇṭukaḷ māttiram āṭci purivatu paḻaṅkāla muṟi. 12vatu āṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil aracaṉ potu veḷiyil kūṭāramaṭittuk viḻāk koṇ ṭāṭuvāṉ. appoḻutu avaṉit kāttu niṟkum nāṟpatāyiram allatu aimpa tāyiram vīrarik kaṭantu vantu avaṉik kolpavaṉ paṭṭattukku varuvāṉ. 1645il iv vaki viḻā oṉṟu naṭipeṟṟatu.
coṉarat (Sonnerat) eṉpavar eḻutiyiruppatu varumāṟu: nāyar pōr vīrar cātiyiṉar. ivarkaḷukkut tamatu cātippeṇkaḷi aṉupa vikkum urimiyuṇṭu. avarkaḷ vītiyiṟ cellumpōtu paṟiyar vilakic cellumpaṭi cattamiṭṭuk koṇṭu celkiṉṟaṉar. paṟiyaṉ oruvaṉ avarkaḷit tīṇṭiṉāl avarkaḷ avaṉik koṉṟuviṭa lām. āṉāl avarkaḷit tīṇṭuvataṟkup paṟiyarukku āṇṭiloru muṟi urimiyuṇṭu. an nāḷil avarkaḷ nāyaril evaṉiyāvatu toṭṭāl avaṉ paṟiyaṉukku aṭimiyāka vēṇṭum. ākavē an nāḷil nāyarkaḷ muṉṉeccarikkiyāka viruppārkaḷ. nāyar eṉpatu nāyaṉ eṉpatiliruntu piṟanta teṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. aracaṉukkup poruḷ koṭuttu kaṇakku, piḷḷi mutaliya paṭṭaṅkaḷum peṟappaṭ ṭaṉa. paṭṭamaḷikkum caṭaṅku ‘tirumuka maḷikkuka’ eṉappaṭum. tiruvitāṅ kūril tampi eṉṉum oru paṭṭamum uṇṭu. itu tiruvitāṅkūr aracaṉiṉ nāyarp putalvaṉukku vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatu. tampimār talip pākiyiṉṟip pallakkil aracaṉ muṉ cellalām. ik kuṭumpaṅkaḷi liruntu aracaṉiṉ maṉivi teriyappaṭu var. nāyarukkuk kātta eṉṉum paṭṭamum uṇṭu. mattiya kālattil cempaṭakarāmaṉ eṉpatu paṭṭat tukku aṭiyāḷamāṉa peyarāka vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. ki.pi. 1500il pārpōcā (Barbosa) eḻutiyiruppatu varumāṟu: aracaṉukku 1000 parivāraṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷukkuc campaḷam uṇṭu. avarkaḷ araṇmaṉiyip perukkutal mutal ellāp paṇiviṭikaḷiyum ceykiṉṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ maṉivi paṇap piḷḷi ammā eṉap paṭuvāḷ. kimāl eṉṉum paṭṭam cilarukku vaḻaṅkum. ip paṭṭam atikārattik kuṟippatu. uṉṉitaṉ, valiyataṉ eṉṉum paṭṭaṅkaḷ tiruvitāṅkūriluḷḷa cila kuṭumpaṅkaḷukku vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. mēṉaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭappeyar mēl eṉṉum aṭi yākap piṟantatu. (mēlavaṉ - mēṉavaṉ - mēṉaṉ) cilar paṇam koṭuttu ip paṭṭattip peṟuki ṟārkaḷ. mēṉaṉ paṭṭam aḷikkappaṭum pōtu paṉi yōlic caṭṭamum eḻuttāṇiyum aḷittal vaḻakkam. iṉṟum piriṭṭiṣ maliyāḷattil ovvoru amcattilum allatu kirāmattilum kaṇak keḻutum mēṉaṉ uṇṭu. mēṉōkki eṉṉum paṭṭam kaṇkāṇip pavaṉ eṉṉum poruḷ taruvatu. ivarkaḷ perumpālum kōyiṟ kaṇakkukaḷi mēṟpārttārkaḷ. nāyarp peṇ pūppaṭintāl mūṉṟu nāḷ tīṭṭuk kāppāḷ.
nāyarp peṇkaḷukku pūppaṭiyumuṉ tālikaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam naṭipeṟum. tālikkaṭṭuk kaliyāṇameṉpatu tēvatācikaḷukkut tālikaṭṭuvatu pōṉṟatu. araca kuṭumpap peṇkaḷukku neṭuṅkāṭi eṉpavaṉ nalla vēḷiyil tāli kaṭṭuvāṉ. avaṉukku ataṟkākak kiṭikkum kūli uṇṭu. maṟṟa vakuppiṉarukku iruvariṉ cātakap poruttamum pārkkappaṭukiṟatu. poruttamuḷḷa piyaṉ maṇavāḷaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. maṇavāḷaṉ taṉatu parivāraṅkaḷuṭaṉ taṉatu vīṭṭi ṉiṉṟum puṟappaṭuvāṉ. peṇ vīṭṭiṉiṉṟum tuvakkap puṟappāṭu naṭakkum. iru pakutiyiṉarum vaḻiyil cantittu peṇ vīṭṭukkuc celvar. peṇṇiṉ cakōtarar maṇavāḷaṉiṉ kālkaḷik kaḻuvu vāṉ. piṉṉar avaṉ peṇṇi aḻittuvantu maṇavāḷaṉiṉ iṭappuṟattil iruttuvāṉ. maṇavāḷaṉ peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil tāli kaṭṭuvāṉ. mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷukku oruvakit tīṭṭuk kākkappaṭum. nālāvatu nāḷ iruvarum āṟṟil muḻukuvārkaḷ. vīṭṭukkut tirumpi varum pōtu katavukaḷ aṭikkappaṭṭirukkum. maṇavāḷaṉ katavit taṭṭi ataṉit tiṟakkumpaṭi ceyvāṉ. piṉ peṇṇiṉ tāyum maṟṟiya peṇkaḷum iruvarukkumiṭiyiliruntu uṇpārkaḷ. piṉ iruvarum pantalukkuc celvārkaḷ. piṉ āṭiyi iraṇṭākak kiḻittu āḷukku oru tuṇṭu koṭukkappaṭum. āṭiyik kiḻippatu kaliyāṇam taḷḷuvatik kuṟikkum. oru maṇavāḷaṉ ettaṉi peṇ kaḷukkum tāli kaṭṭalām. perumpālum maṇavāḷaṉ pirāmaṇaṉāka iruppāṉ. ivaṉ tāṉ ceyyum kaṭamikaḷukkuk kūli peṟṟuc celvāṉ. peṇ virumpiṉāl nālāvatu nāḷ tāliyik kaḻaṟṟi viṭalām. cila iṭaṅkaḷil tāli kaṭṭiyavaṉē campantattukku urimi yuṭiyavaṉ eṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. tāli kaṭṭumpōtu ammāc cām pāṭṭu eṉṉum oruvakip pāṭṭu pāṭappaṭum. tāli miṉṉu eṉappaṭum. teṉ mēṟku mūliyil kaṉṉikkāl naṭappaṭum. kaliyāṇap pantal neṟkatirkaḷāl alaṅkarikkappaṭṭirukkum ataṟkuk katir maṇṭapameṉpatu peyar. atu mullippantal eṉappaṭuvatuṇṭu. maṇanāḷukku mutal nāḷ naṭattappaṭum viruntu ayaṇi ūṉ eṉappaṭum. pirāmaṇatti peṇṇiṉ iṭatu kiyil kāppuk kaṭṭi cupattiri vēli eṉṉum pāṭṭup pāṭuvāḷ. kaliyāṇak kālattil katikaḷi, ōṭṭam tuḷḷal mutaliyaṉa naṭipeṟum. pirāyamāṉa peṇṇik kaṉṉi aḻippatu tīṭṭāṉa ceyal eṉak karutappaṭṭatu. ākavē pūppaṭinta peṇkaḷik kaṉṉi yaḻikkumpaṭi peṇṇiṉ tāymār iḷiñari irantu vēṇṭuvar.
kiṭakkiṟateṉpatu campantattukku maṟṟoru peyar. peṇkaḷ pūppaṭivatu tiraṇṭukuḷi eṉappaṭum. nāyarp peṇkaḷ karuvaṭintu ēḻāvatu mātattil puḷikkuṭi eṉṉum caṭaṅku purivar. vīṭṭiṉ vaṭakkuc ciṟaku vaṭakkiṇi eṉṟum teṉkuc ciṟaku teṟkiṇi eṉavum paṭum. naṭuvē uḷḷa muṟṟam naṭumuṟṟam eṉappaṭum. nāṭṭukkatti pīcāṉ katti eṉavum tūkkum viḷakku tūkkuviḷakkeṉavum paṭum. āṇ kuḻanti piṟantāl nilattil teṉṉōliyāl aṭippārkaḷ. irupat tēḻāvatu nāḷ kuḻantikkup pāl parukkip peyariṭappaṭum. cōṟūṭ ṭum caṭaṅku cōṟūṇ eṉappaṭum. nāḻiyil arici nirappi vittal niṟiccuvippu eṉappaṭum. purōkitaṉ cāntikkāraṉ eṉavum upātti yāyaṉ eṉavum eḻuttaccaṉ eṉavum paṭuvāṉ. pāl kuṭikkup piṉ taṭṭāṉi aḻittu vantu poṉ kampiyiṉāl kātu kuttappaṭum. appoḻutu nila viḷakkuk koḷutti vikkappaṭum. maliyāḷa vīṭu nāṟpuṟam eṉappaṭum. mattiya kālattil vācalil patipuram eṉṉum kāvaṟ koṭṭil iruntatu. ippoḻutu av viṭattil vēyappaṭṭa koṭṭiluṇṭu. nāyarp peṇkaḷ mārpi maṟippatilli. mārpi maṟippatu tāḻnta cātikku aṟikuṟi, 1740il maliyāḷattukku vanta eṭvarṭ īvs eṉpavar, “cātāraṇa vayal vēli ceypavarkaḷiṉ peṇkaḷ mutal araca kuṭumpap peṇkaḷ variyum arikkumēl uṭiyillātavarkaḷāka virukkiṉṟaṉar eṉak kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. nāyarp peṇkaḷ nāka paṭam eṉṉum oru vaki māliyiyaṇivar. mūkkil mūkkutti iṭuvar. atil tūkkappaṭuvatu nattu eṉappaṭum. ari yil poṉ veḷḷi āparaṇaṅkaḷaṇivar. oṭṭiyāṇam kaccāpuram eṉappaṭum. maṇamākātu iṟanta peṇkaḷiṉ maraṇam kaḻiccāvu, kaṉṉic cāvu eṉappaṭum. peṇkaḷ kaṇṇukku mi tīṭṭuvar. ūñcal uḻiñcāl eṉappaṭum.
nāyāṭi: maliyāḷattil mikat tāḻnta cātiyiṉar nāyāṭika ḷāvar. nīrnāyiṉ iṟicciyi uṇpatāl avarkaḷukku ip peyar uṇṭāyiṟṟu. munnūṟu yārukkuḷ nāyāṭi vantāl pirāmaṇaṉ kuḷittup pūṇūl māṟṟik koḷḷavēṇṭum. peṇkaḷ nāyaṭiccikaḷ eṉappaṭuvārkaḷ. ciṟuvar molāyar eṉṟum ciṟumiyar maṉicciyar eṉavum paṭuvar. ivarkaḷukku makkaḷ tāyam uṇṭu. ivarkaḷ tēṉ, maṭṭippāl mutaliyaṉa eṭuppar. ovvoru nāyāṭiyum ōṇap peru nāḷukku nampūtiri illattukku eṭṭu yāruḷḷa (kecam) nālukayiṟum nāyar vīṭṭukku iraṇṭu kayiṟum koṭukka vēṇṭum. avaṟṟukkuk kūliyāka nāyāṭi nel peṟuvāṉ. kayiṟukaḷ āṭu māṭukaḷ kaṭṭavum taṇṇīr iṟikkavum utavum. āṇ kuḻantikkut tantiyiṉ takappaṉ peyar iṭappaṭum. maṇappeṇ taṉiyāka iṭappaṭṭa oru kuṭiciyuḷ iruppāḷ. paruvamaṭinta iḷiñar kuṭiciyic cuṟṟi niṉṟu kūt tāṭuvar. avarkaḷ ellōrum kiyil vittirukkum taṭiyik kūri vaḻiyākak kuṭicikkuḷ pōṭuvar. peṇ entat taṭiyi eṭukkiṟāḷō at taṭikkuriyavaṉē avaḷ kaṇavaṉāṉavaṉ. ivarkaḷiṉ kaṭavuḷar mallar, malivaṉ, paṟakkuṭṭi mutaliyōrāvar. ivarkaḷiṉ cōtiṭar paṟiya vakuppiṉarāvar. oruvaṉukkuk kaṇ tiṭṭi allatu pēyk kuṟipāṭu iruntāl uppu, miḷakāy, puḷi, eṇṇey, kaṭuku, tēṅkāy, kācu eṉpavikaḷi oru ēṉattiliṭṭu avaṉuṭiya taliyi mūṉṟu muṟi cuṟṟiya piṉ avi nāyāṭikkuk koṭukkappaṭum. cōṉakarāka māṟiya nāyāṭikaḷ toppiyiṭṭa nāyāṭi eṉappaṭuvar.
nālillakkārar: mukkuvar.
nālki(Nalke): ivarkaḷ kaṉṉaṭattil vāḻum kūṭimuṭiyum kuṭi ceyyum makkaḷ. ivarkaḷ hōliyar allatu paṟiyar eṉappaṭuvar. pēyk kūttāṭuvar. peṇkaḷ pūppaṭintapiṉ maṇa muṭippar. ivarkaḷiṉ nāṭṭāṇmikkāraṉ kuṟikkāraṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ. avaṉ purōkitaṉāka viruntu maṇaṅkaḷi naṭattuvāṉ. iṟantavar kaḷic cuṭṭa allatu putitta iṭattil maṇmēṭu ceyyappaṭu kiṉṟatu.
pañcamar: aintāvatu kulattavarum paṟiyarum avarkaḷ pōṉṟa cātiyiṉarum pañcamareṉappaṭuvar.
paṭakar allatu vaṭukar: ivarkaḷ micūriliruntu ceṉṟu nīlakiriyiṟ kuṭiyēṟiyavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ kaṉṉaṭac citivāṉa moḻi pēcuvar.
paṭṭaṇavaṉ: kiḻakkuk kaṭaṟkariyil kiruṭṭiṇā mutal tañcāvūr variyiluḷḷa mīṉ piṭikārar ippeyar peṟuvar. kariyār eṉṉum peyarum ivarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkum. cila cempaṭavar āriyar, aiyāyirat talivar, āriya nāṭṭuc ceṭṭi, accu veḷḷāḷaṉ, karittuṟi veḷḷāḷaṉ, varuṇakula veḷḷāḷaṉ, kurukulavamicam mutaliyavaṟṟit tamatu kulap peyarkaḷāka vaḻaṅkuvar. ivarkaḷ perumpālum tammip piḷḷi eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. paṭṭaṇavar cempaṭavarukkuk kīḻp paṭṭavareṉak karutappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ ciṟanta teyvaṅkaḷ kuṭṭi āṇṭavaṉ, kuṭṭi āṇṭavaṉiṉ parivāram, cempu vīrappaṉ, mīṉōṭum piḷḷi eṉpaṉa. tañcāvūril ivarkaḷ kaṭavuḷ pāvāṭirāyaṉ. ivarkaḷ ik kaṭavuḷiyum valiyiyum vaṇaṅkuvar. maṇaṟkumpaṅkaḷ avarkaḷ kaṭavuḷarik kuṟippaṉavākum. talimik kāraṉ ecamāṉaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. ivaṉukkut taṇṭakkāraṉ eṉṉum tuṇivaṉ uṇṭu. ivarkaḷiṉ iḻavu collic celpavaṉ calavāti eṉappaṭuvaṉ.
paṭṭunūṟkāraṉ: ivarkaḷ piṟanāṭukaḷiliruntu vantu tamiḻ nāṭṭil taṅkiya necavāḷar. ivarkaḷiṉ āti iṭam kucarāttu. kumarākuptāviṉ paṭṭiyattil (ki.pi. 473) ivarkaḷ ‘paṭṭavāyāka’ eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. itu paṭṭunūṟkārar eṉpataṉ camakkiruta moḻi peyarppu. irāṇi maṅkammāḷiṉ paṭṭiyattilum ivarkaḷ paṭṭu nūṟkārar eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. piṟkālattil ivarkaḷ tammic curāṭṭirar eṉak kūṟikkoṇṭaṉar. ivarkaḷ tammip pirāmaṇar eṉavum kūṟikkoḷvar. kumārakuptaṉiṉ cācaṉattil ivarkaḷ pōr vīrarum necavāḷarumeṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. paṭṭunūṟkārar teluṅku moḻiyum pēcuvar. itaṉāl ivarkaḷ nīṇṭa kālam teluṅku nāṭṭilum vāḻntārkaḷ eṉat terikiṟatu. ivarkaḷ pirāmaṇarip pōla nūlaṇivar. peṇkaḷ pūppaṭiyumuṉ maṇa muṭippar. ivarkaḷiṉ maṇak kiriyi peritum pirāmaṇaruṭiyatu pōṉṟatu. peṇṇiṉ kaḻuttil kaṭṭappaṭum tāli poṭṭu eṉap paṭum. nālāvatu nāḷ nākavalli eṉṉum caṭaṅku naṭipeṟum. piṟantu patiṉōrāvatu nāḷ piḷḷikkup peyariṭappaṭum. eṭṭāvatu piḷḷi āṇāka viruntāl kiruṣṇā eṉṉum peyariṭappaṭum. itu kiruṣṇaṉ vacutēvarukku eṭṭāvatu piḷḷiyākap piṟantāreṉṉum aitīkattip paṟṟiyatu. ivarkaḷukku maraṇattīṭṭu pattunāḷ. vitavikaḷ tali moṭṭi yaṭippatilli; poṭṭik kaḻaṟṟiviṭuvārkaḷ. paṭṭunūṟkārariṭiyē maṟṟavarkaḷ viḷaṅkamāṭṭāta viyāpāra moḻi uṇṭu.
paṇikkar: paṇikkaṉ eṉṉum peyar ampaṭṭaṉ, kammāḷaṉ, māṟaṉ, nāyar, pāṇaṉ, paṟiyaṉ mutaliyavarkaḷukkiṭiyil vaḻaṅkum. maturi, tirunelvēlip pakutikaḷil paṇikkar cilar kāṇappaṭukiṉ ṟaṉar. avarkaḷiṟ cilar ampaṭṭarum cāṇārumāvar. cilar necavut toḻil purivar. ippoḻutu paṇikkar tammi illam vēḷāḷar eṉpar. avarkaḷ tamatu peyarip piḷḷi eṉat tirutti vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉar.
paṇicavaṉ: paṇicavaṉ eṉpatu paṇi ceykiṉṟavaṉ eṉpataṉ tiripu. ivarkaḷ iḻavu aṟivippavarkaḷāvar. cāri allatu ekkāḷam ūtuvar. ivarkaḷil valaṅki, iṭaṅki eṉa iruvakip pirivuka ḷuṇṭu. paṇicavaṉ caṅku ūtikkoṇṭu piṇattukkup piṉṉāl celvāṉ. iṟantavar kaṇṇiyamuṭiyavarāṉāl kompu ūtuvāṉ. maṟavarkaḷukkuḷ ampaṭṭaṉ iḻavu aṟivikkac celvāṉ. tañcāvūri lum teṉ ārkkāṭṭilum paṇicavarkaḷiṉ vēliyic ceyvōr nōkkāṉ eṉappaṭuvar. tirunelvēlip pācavariṉ kōyilkaḷil nākacuram vācittal, tēvaṭiyāṭkaḷukku nāṭṭiyam paḻakkutal mutaliya vēlikaḷiyum ivarkaḷ ceyvar. cilar accu vēliyum ceyvar. accu vēliyeṉpatu necavāḷar pāvōṭum accuc ceyvatu.
paṇiyaṉ: ivarkaḷ maliyāḷattilum nīlakirip pakutiyilum kāṇappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ kāṇiyāḷariṉ aṭimikaḷāvar. ivarkaḷ uyarnta iṭaṅkaḷil kūḷi eṉṉum teyvatti vittu vaḻipaṭuvar. av vakit tiṭar kuḷittari eṉappaṭum. ivarkaḷiṉ ciṟanta poḻutu pōkku ūñcalāṭutal. paṇiyariṟ cilar tamatu maṇikkaṭṭukaḷilum kaḻuttilum mantira mōtiya nūlik kaṭṭiyiruppar.
paṇṭāram: ivarkaḷ pirāmaṇarallāta kurumār. tiruttaṇi kiyil paṇṭāraṅkaḷ perituṅ kāṇappaṭuvar. civa maṭaṅkaḷiṉ talivarum paṇṭāraṅkaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. paṇṭāraṅkaḷ iliṅkantarip par. ivarkaḷiṟ cilar kōyilkaḷil māli kaṭṭutal, paṇ ōtutal, mutaliya paṇikaḷ ceyvar. avarkaḷ ōtuvār, meykāval eṉṉum peyarkaḷum peṟuvar. ivarkaḷil illaṟattār, tuṟavikaḷ eṉa iru piriviṉaruṇṭu. maṭaṅkaḷukkut talivarāyirukkum caṉṉatikaḷ tuṟavi vakuppiṉarāvar. malikaḷilum kukikaḷilum marappontukaḷilum vāḻum oruvakik kāṭṭuc cātiyiṉarum paṇṭāraṅkaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. avarkaḷ maliyāḷatti aṭutta malikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.
paṇṭitaṉ: mēṟkōṭṭu niyāyātipatikkut tuṇiyākap paṇṭita ṉiruppāṉ. 1862il ip patavi oḻikkappaṭṭatu. paṇṭitaṉ ampaṭṭaṉiṉ paṭṭap peyarumākum. tamiḻnāṭṭil mattuvap pirāmaṇarum paṇṭita reṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷiṟ palar kōṭukaḷiṟ paṇṭitarāka viruntaṉar. ōriya cātiyiṉariṉ cōtiṭarum paṇṭita reṉappaṭuvar.
patiṉeṭṭāṉ: itu maliyāḷattil taccarukku vaḻaṅkum peyar.
pattirāḷu: ivarkaḷ teluṅku pēcum vāttiyakārar. ivarkaḷ āṭuvōr pāṭuvōrāvar.
paravaṉ: paravar tamatu ātiyiṭam ayōtti eṉak karutukiṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ teṉṉintiyak kaṭalōraṅkaḷilum ilaṅkiyilum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ mukkiya iṭam tūttukkuṭi. teṉkiḻakkuk karikaḷil vāḻum paravar perumpālum kiṟittuva kattōlikka matat tiṉarāvar. ivarkaḷukkup pōrccukkēyariṉ peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkum. ivarkaḷ pirāṉcis cēviyarāl kiṟittuva matattukkut tiruppappaṭṭa varkaḷāvar. ivarkaḷiṉ urimivaḻi makkaḷ tāyam. mattiya tiruvitāṅ kūril ivarkaḷ maramēṟuvar; mīṉ piṭippar; kiṟittuvarukkum tāḻttap paṭṭōrukkum tuṇi veḷuppar. peṇkaḷ cippiyic cuṭṭu cuṇṇāmpu ceyvar.
parivāram: maṟavar akampaṭiyāruḷ oru piriviṉar. ivarkaḷil ciṉṉa ūḻiyam, periya ūḻiyam eṉa iru pirivukaḷ uṇṭu. ivarkaḷuḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāḷ peṇṇukkut tālikaṭṭuvāḷ. contac cātiyāruṭaṉ allatu camīṉtāruṭaṉ peṇkaḷ cērkki vit tiruppatu kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭuvatilli. camīṉtārkaḷukku avvāṟu piṟakkum piḷḷikaḷik kaṇavar ēṟṟukkoḷvar. av vakip piḷḷikaḷ periya kamkaḷattār eṉappaṭuvar.
paratēci: ivarkaḷ oruvakip piccikkārar. ip peyar kocci veḷḷiyūtarukkum peyarāka vaḻaṅkum.
palicakkollaṉ: maliyāḷattila kēṭakam ceyyum kollar ip peyar peṟuvar.
paliyaṉ allatu poliyaṉ: ivarkaḷ paḻanimaliyil vāḻum kuṭikaḷ. ivarkaḷ oruvakit tamiḻ pēcuvar. peritum kāṭukaḷilum malikaḷilum alintu tirivar. palar mūlikikaḷik koṇṭu nōykaḷi māṟṟuvar. pāmpuviṭattip pōkkutaṟku naṟuvalli vēri vittiruppar. vilaṅkukaḷip poṟik kiṭaṅkukaḷilum aṭārkaḷilum akappaṭuttuvar. kaṅkak koṭi iliyik kacakkit taṇṇīril eṟivārkaḷ. uṭaṉē mīṉkaḷ taṇṇīril mitakkum. ivarkaḷiṉ kaṭavuḷ māyāṇṭi.
pallavarāyaṉ: itu pallavar talivaṉukku vaḻaṅkum peyar. ōccaril oru piriviṉarukkum pallavarāyaṉ eṉṉum peyar vaḻaṅkum.
paḷḷaṉ: ivarkaḷ uḻavut toḻil puriyum vēliyāṭkaḷ. ivarkaḷ muṟkālattil veḷḷāḷarukku aṭimikaḷāka iruntārkaḷ. maturip paḷḷariṉ atikāri kuṭumpaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. kōyamuttūril paḷḷariṉ atikāri paṭṭakkāraṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. avaṉukkut tuṇi yāka vuḷḷavaṉ ōṭum piḷḷi eṉappaṭuvāṉ. tiruccirāppaḷḷiyil paḷḷariṉ atikāri nāṭṭumūppaṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ. ivarkaḷukku vaṇṇār ampaṭṭarkaḷuṇṭu.
paḷḷi: teluṅku nāṭṭil mīṉ piṭippōr, uḻutoḻil ceyvōr eṉap paḷḷikaḷil iru pirivukaḷ uṇṭu. mīṉ piṭippōr mīṉ paḷḷikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. paḷḷikaḷ cila iṭaṅkaḷil tammi ireṭ ṭikaḷ eṉak kūṟuvar. mīṉ piṭippōr akkā, tēvarālu eṉṉum teyvaṅkaḷi vaḻipaṭuvar.
paḷḷi allatu vaṉṉiyaṉ: paḷḷikaḷ tām vaṉṉiya kula araca kulattiṉar eṉak kūṟuvar. tiruvitāṅkūr aracaruḷ oruvarākiya kulacēkara āḻvār taṅkaḷ cātiyaracaṉ eṉak kūṟuvar. ceṉṉiyil tiruvallikkēṇiyil pārttacārati kōyiṟ paṭṭiyattiṉpaṭi atu paḷḷarāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu. aṅku paḷḷikaḷ kulacēkara āḻvārukkuk kurupūci naṭattuvārkaḷ. milāppūrc civaṉ kōyilil paḷḷikaḷ kaṟpūram koḷuttik kāṭṭum urimi peṟṟiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kōyil tamakku uriyatāka viruntateṉṟum piṟkālattil tām ciṟitu ciṟitāka urimiyi iḻantu viṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. kaṟpūram koḷuttum urimiyippaṟṟi camīpattil vivātam naṭantu vicāraṇi mēṟkōṭṭukku vantatu. paḷḷikaḷ cārpākat tīrppaḷikkap paṭṭatu. kāñcīpurattil ēkāmparanātar kōyiṟ kōpuramoṉṟu paḷḷi kōpurameṉappaṭum. aṅkuḷḷa paḷḷikaḷ atu tamakkuriya teṉṟu kūṟi ataṉi āṇṭutōṟum paḻutu pārppar. citampara ālayattik kaṭṭiyavaṉ iraṇiyavaṉmaṉ (ki. pi. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭu). avaṉuṭiya vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷtām eṉṟu citamparattukkuk kiḻakkē nāṉku mil tūrattiluḷḷa oru paḷḷik kuṭumpattiṉar urimi koṇṭāṭuvar. paḷḷip polikariṟ cilar “akkiṉi kutiriyēṟiya rāyarāvutta miṇṭa nayiṉār” (irācarāvuttari veṉṟu akkiṉik kutiri ēṟiya) eṉṟu peyar peṟuvar. teṉṉārkkāṭṭil kumālam eṉṉum iṭattil paḷḷikkuc ciṟīṉivāca ālayamuṇṭu. aṅku paḷḷikaḷ kōyilic cuṟṟi vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ kōyiṟ paṭṭarip pōla uṭuppar. paḷḷip peṇkaḷip paṭṭarmār maṇappar. āṉāl tamatu peṇkaḷip paḷḷikkuk koṭukkamāṭṭārkaḷ. cēlam pakutiyil ōlip paḷḷi, nākavaṭampaḷḷi eṉa iru piriviṉaruṇṭu. ivi avarkaḷ aṇiyum ōli, nākavaṭam mutaliyaṉa paṟṟi vanta peyarkaḷ. paḷḷikaḷum pēric ceṭṭikaḷum maṉṉār cāmiyi vaṇaṅkuvar. kirāmat teyvaṅkaḷ ēḻukumpam allatu karakaṅkaḷāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum. ivarkaḷuḷ piccik kārar nōkkāṉ eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ talimikkāraṉ periya taṉakkāraṉ allatu nāṭṭāṇmikkāra ṉeṉappaṭuvāṉ. vitavi kaḷiṉ maṟumaṇam naṭuvīṭṭuttāli eṉappaṭum. talippūppu aṭinta peṇ muḻukiya piṉ avaḷip palakimēl irutti avaḷ muṉṉāl piṭṭuvittu ārātti kāṭṭappaṭum.
paṟiyaṉ: 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil coṉarat (Sonnerat) eṉpavar paṟiyarip paṟṟi eḻutiyiruppatu varumāṟu: maṟṟavarkaḷōṭu pēcum pōtu paṟiyaṉ taṉatu vāyik kiyiṉāl pottikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. avaṉ vītiyiṟ pōyk koṇṭiruntāl avaṉ orupakkam otuṅki niṉṟu maṟṟavarkaḷic cella vaḻiviṭa vēṇṭum. avaṉi evarāvatu tīṇṭiṉāl avar uṭaṉē kuḷittut tōyntu tīṭṭip pōkkikkoḷḷa vēṇṭum. pirāmaṇar ivarkaḷip pārttal kūṭātu. ivarkaḷuḷ pala pirivukaḷuṇṭu. kōliyar necavu ceyvar. vaḷḷuvar mantira vittikkārarākavum, kurumārākavum vēli ceyvar, nūlumaṇivar. paṟiyar aṭikkum paṟi uṟumi eṉappaṭum. ivarkaḷiṉ vaṇṇāṉ pōtarāyaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. pēlūril (Belur) uḷḷa kōyiluḷ mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷukku uḷḷē cella ivarkaḷukku urimi yuṇṭu. mēl kōṭṭiyil mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷukkup pirāmaṇaruṭaṉ ātimūlattukku (karuppak kirakattukkuc) cella irāmāṉucarāl ivarkaḷukku atikāram koṭukkappaṭṭateṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu 1799 mutal ivarkaḷ koṭimarattukku appāl cellātapaṭi taṭukkap paṭṭārkaḷ. ceṅkaṟpaṭṭiluḷḷa ciṟiperumputtūril tiruvārūriṟ pōṉṟa urimi paṟiyarukku ṇṭu. veṭṭiyāṉ, taliyāri, taṇṭāci, tōṭṭi mutaliya kirāma uttiyōkaṅkaḷ paṟic cātiyiṉarukku uriya tākum. kāñcīpuram, kumpakōṇam, ciṟīvilliputtūr mutaliya iṭaṅ kaḷil paṟiyar maṟṟavarkaḷōṭu vaṭam piṭittut tēr iḻuppar. kirāma tēvatikaḷukku maṭi pōṭumpōtu pūcāri paṟiyaṉiṉ maṇikkaṭ ṭil mañcaḷ nūlkaṭṭi avaṉi ellōrukkum muṉṉāl ūrvalat til varaviṭuvatu vaḻakkam. maḻiyillāta kālattil paṟiyar koṭum pāvi kaṭṭiyiḻuppar. pirāmaṇar varumuṉ vaḷḷuvar aracarkaḷukkuk kurukkaḷāka viruntār eṉṟu sṭūvāṭ (Stuwart) kūṟiyuḷḷār. avar ki.pi. 9ām nūṟṟāṇṭil veṭṭappaṭṭa paṭṭayamoṉṟi itaṟku ātāramākak kāṭṭuvar. atil “ciṟī vaḷḷuvaṉ puvaṉavaṉākiya uvaccaṉ tiṉamum aṟuvarik koṇṭu vēli ceyvittu ālayak kaṭami kaḷip pārkka vēṇṭuma” eṉap poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭa irācarācaṉiṉ kalveṭṭil paṟiyar uḻavu, necavu eṉa iru piriviṉarākaviruntaṉar eṉak kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷ ḷatu. tiruvārūril naṭakkum periya viḻāvil paṟiyaṉ yāṉi mītu ēṟi yiruntu cuvāmikkuc cāmari vīcuvāṉ. ceṉṉiyil jāṭs ṭavuṉil naṭakkum ēkāṭṭa viḻāvil vikkiraka ttiṉ kaḻuttil paṟiyaṉ tālikaṭṭu vatu vaḻakkam. nilaṅkaḷiṉ elliyip paṟiyaṉ naṉku aṟivāṉ. elliyip paṟṟip piṇakku uṇṭāṉāl taliyil nīrk kuṭam vittu avaṉi elliyic cuṟṟivarumpaṭi ceytal iṉṟum cila iṭaṅkaḷil vaḻakkākavuḷḷatu. itaṉāl maṟṟac cātiyiṉarilum pārkkap paṟiyaṉ neṭuṅkālam nāṭṭil vāḻntu varukiṉṟāṉeṉṟu aṟiyappaṭukiṉṟatu. paṟiyarirukkumiṭam cēri eṉappaṭum. pirāmaṇaṉ toṭuvatāl tamakkut tīṭṭu uṇṭākiṟateṉap paṟiyar karutuvar. pirāmaṇaṉ cērikkum nuḻintāl avaṉit taliyil cāṇit taṇṇīr ūṟṟit turattuvar. micūrp pirāmaṇar hōliyariṉ cērik kūṭṭākac ceṉṟāl tamakku atiṭṭam uṇṭākum eṉak karutuvar. pirāmaṇaṉ avvāṟu ceṉṟāl hōliyar tiraṇṭu avaṉic ceruppālaṭittu turattuvar. muṟkālattil cākumpaṭi aṭippar. pattut talimuṟikku muṉ paṟiyar necavukkārarākavum, mariyātikkuriyavar kaḷākavumiruntārkaḷ. ivarkaḷiṟ kaṇṇiyamuṭiyavaṉ paṇakkāraṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ. teṉṉārkkāṭṭup paṟiyaruḷ periya nāṭṭāṉ, ciṉṉa nāṭṭāṉ eṉṉum talimikkārar uṇṭu. vitavi tāli tarippa tilli. paṟiyar perumpālum ēḻu kaṉṉiyammāvi vaṇaṅkuvar. tamiḻ teluṅkup paṟiyar ellammā eṉṉum teyvatti vaṇaṅkuvar. ivarkaḷ teyvattik kuṟikkum cilimītu eṇṇey ūṟṟi mañcaḷ pūci kuṅkumam tūvi mālikaḷāl alaṅkarittu vaṇaṅkuvar. periya pāḷiyattil (ceṉṉiyiliruntu patiṉāṟu mil) pavāṉi ammaṉ kōyiluṇṭu. iṅku paṟiyar, paḷḷikaḷ, cakkiliyar mutalāṉōr perumpālum vaḻipaṭuvōrāvar. aṅku avarkaḷ āṭukaḷip pali yiṭuvatōṭu nirvāṇamāka vēppili uṭi uṭuttuk koṇṭu kōyilic cuṟṟi varuvar. vaḷḷuvar maṟṟap paṟiya rōṭu tirumaṇam ceytu koḷvatilli. tiruvitāṅkūr, kocci mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil tirumaṇattukkup pantaṟkāl niṟuttutal paṟiya rāl ceyyappaṭum. paṟiyaṉ pirāmaṇaṉukku 128 aṭi tūrattil niṟṟal vēṇṭum. puliyaṉ itaṟku iraṭṭit tūrattil niṟṟal vēṇṭum. paṟiyaṉ maṉiviyiṉ piḷḷip pēṟṟukkup piṉ ēḻu nāṭkaḷukkuc cōṟu uṇṇātu paḻaṅkaḷiyum, kiḻaṅkukaḷiyum uṇpāṉ. nāñcil nāṭṭup paṟiyarukkuc cottu uṇṭu. ivarkaḷ aracarukkup paṇaṅ koṭuttup paṭṭaṅkaḷ peṟṟuḷḷārkaḷ. pāṇṭippaṟiyaṉ cāmpu vaṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ.
pāṇaṉ: maturiyilum tirunelvēliyilum ivarkaḷ tiyal vēli ceyvar. ampaṭṭarum vaṇṇārum ivar vīṭukaḷil uṇ ṇārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ kōyilkaḷuḷ nuḻiyalām. maliyāḷap pāṇar pēyk kūttāṭuvōrāvar.
pāṇō: ivarkaḷ kañcam mākāṇattil kāṇappaṭum neca vāḷar. cūṭiya nākapuriyilum. oricāvilum necavu kūṭimuṭital mutaliya vēlikaḷ ceyyum pāṇa eṉṉum oru cātiyiṉarum kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ maṇa vīṭukaḷilum, piṇa vīṭukaḷilum , kōyil kaḷilum vāttiyam cēvippar. pāṇō oruvaṉ koṇṭar vakuppup peṇṇōṭu viyapicāram ceytāl avaṉ peṇṇiṉ kaṇavaṉukku erumi, āṭu, paṉṟi, oru kūṭi nel, oru rūpāy, oru cumi pāṉi mutaliyavaṟṟik koṭukkavēṇṭum. koṇṭarp peṇkaḷip pōlavē pāṇōp peṇkaḷum avarkaḷukkāka otukkappaṭṭuḷḷa taṉiyiṭattil irākkālattil nittiri koḷvar.
pāṇṭiyaṉ: ampaṭṭaṉ, kammāḷaṉ, occaṉ, paḷḷaṉ, vaṇṇāṉ, vēḷāḷaṉ mutaliyavarkaḷiṭiyē itu peyarāka vaḻaṅkum. maliyāḷattil īḻavar pāṇṭi eṉappaṭuvar.
pārattuvācar: itu oru pirāmaṇa kōttirattiṉ peyar.
picārāṭi: ivarkaḷ ampalavācikaḷil oru piriviṉar. ivarkaḷ kōyilkaḷil pūmāli kaṭṭip paṇiviṭi purivar. peṇkaḷukkup pūppu aṭiyumuṉ tāli kaṭṭuk kaliyāṇam naṭakkum. maṇamakaṉ, maṇamakaḷ eṉpavarkaḷiṉ kiḷi iṇippatu maṇattil mukkiya caṭaṅkākum.
piṭāraṉ: ivarkaḷ ampalavācikaḷil oru piriviṉar. pattira kāḷiyi vaṇaṅkuvar. pāmpu piṭippōrum piṭārar eṉappaṭuvar.
piramacāktā: ivarkaḷ pirāmaṇaruḷ mattiyāṉap paṟiyar eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ mattiyāṉam mutal oru maṇi vari vīṭṭukku veḷiyē niṉṟu piṉ kuḷittut tīṭṭup pōkkik koḷvar.
pirāmaṇaṉ: teṉṉintiyap pirāmaṇariṟ pala pirivukaḷuṇṭu. avarkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷum paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷum vēṟupaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivarkaḷ tām piramāviṉ mukattiṉiṉṟum piṟantavarkaḷeṉa nampukiṉṟa ṉar. irukku vētikaḷ, cāmavētikaḷ, yacur vētikaḷ eṉa mūṉṟu pirivil ivarkaḷ aṭaṅkuvar. ivi camayakkiriyi toṭarpāṉa pirivukaḷ. tām vāḻum iṭaṅkaḷil vaḻaṅkum moḻikaḷiyē ivarkaḷum vaḻaṅku var. attiri, piruku, vatiṣṭar, kutamar, kāciyapar mutalāyi ṉōrit tamatu kōttira mutalvarākak koḷvar. cilar akattiyar, aṅkīrar, attiri, piruku, kāciyapar, vatiṣṭar, kutamar eṉavum tamatu kōttira mutalvarik koḷvar. pirāmaṇar pañcat tirāviṭar, kuṭar eṉa iru perum piriviṉarākap pirikkappaṭuvar. kuṭar māmicamuṇpar. oriyā, koṅkaṇi moḻikaḷip pēcuvōrallāta teṉṉintiyap pirāmaṇarellōrum pañcat tirāviṭarkaḷāvar. ivarkaḷ tamiḻ, teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, marāṭṭi, kucarāttip pirāmaṇarka ḷeṉap pirikkap paṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. teluṅku pēcum civālip pirāmaṇar karunāṭakap pirāmaṇaril aṭaṅkuvar. paṭṭar, nampūtirikaḷ, tirāviṭap pirāmaṇaric cērntōrāvar. camayat toṭarpākap pirāmaṇar viṇavarumallar, civarumallar. smārttar piramamē uyirkaḷ eṉa nampuvōr. pirāmaṇac ciṟuvar eṭṭu vayatukku muṉ pūṇūl tarittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. pūṇūl tarikkumpōtu ciṟiya māṉtōl tuṇṭu oṉṟu nūliṟ kaṭṭappaṭum. aimpatu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ viyapicārikaḷ kaḻuti yil vāṟ pakkattip pārkkumpaṭi irutti kirāma vītikaḷil koṇṭu cellappaṭṭārkaḷ. potu makkaḷ erukkampū māliyi avaḷ kaḻutti liṭṭārkaḷ. viyapicārikaḷāṉa uppiliyap peṇkaḷit taliyil oru kūṭi maṇṇic cumantukoṇṭu marattic cuṟṟivarac ceyvatu vaḻakku. ivarkaḷiṉ piṇak kiriyi karuṭa purāṇattit taḻuviyatu.
pillavar: ivarkaḷ tuḷup pēcum, kaḷḷiṟakkum teṉ kaṉṉaṭar. tamiḻar ilaṅki mītu paṭieṭutta pōtu ilaṅki makkaḷ palar tiruvitāṅkūrilum, mēṟkuk karip pakutikaḷilum kuṭiyēṟiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ tiruvitāṅkūrukkuc ceṉṟapōtu teṟkiluḷḷatu eṉṉum poruḷ tarum teṉṉiyiyum koṇṭu ceṉṟārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ tīyar allatu īḻavar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. tīvār eṉpatu tīyar eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. iv vakuppiṉarē piṉpu pillavar eṉappaṭṭārkaḷāka lām. teṅku ilaṅkiyiliruntu vantateṉpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭāka perippuḷūs, maliyāḷattiliruntu mēl nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟa poruḷkaḷuḷ tēṅkāyik kuṟippiṭātiruppatākum. cīriya kiṟitta variṉ ceppup paṭṭiyattāl tīyar payiriṭuvōrāyiruntaṉareṉat terikiṟatu. pillavarkaḷum ivarkaḷic cērntavarkaḷākalām. ivar kaḷukku ampaṭṭaṉ purōkitaṉākaviruntu maṇakkiriyikaḷ cey vāṉ. ivarkaḷukkup peṇ vaḻi urimiyuṇṭu.
piḷḷi: itu vēḷāḷariṉ paṭṭappeyar. itu ippoḻutu akampaṭiyāṉ, ampalakkāraṉ. kōvalaṉ, iṭiyaṉ, nāyar, nōk kāṉ, paṇicavaṉ, paṇikkaṉ, paṟiyaṉ, cāyakkāraṉ, tēvatāci vakuppiṉ āṇkaḷukkum vaḻaṅkum. airōppiyariṉ camiyaṟkārarāṉa ‘paṭlar’ kaḷum piḷḷi eṉṉum peyari vaḻaṅkuvar. vēḷāḷar eṉac collik koḷḷum kuṟavarum itaṉi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉar.
pūlāṉ: ivarkaḷ tiruvitāṅkūril kuṭiyēṟiya tamiḻ ampaṭṭar.
pūḻiācāri: pūḻi(maṇ)yil vēli ceyyum maliyāḷak kammāḷaril oru piriviṉar ip peyar peṟuvar.
puḷḷuvaṉ: maliyāḷattil cōtiṭar, mantira vittikkāraṉ, pūcāri, pāmpukkākkaḷil(pāmpuk kōyilirukkum cōli) pāṭuvōr ip peyar peṟuvar. ivarkaḷ caṭṭiyiṉ aṭiyi uṭittu veḷip pakkattil tōl pōrtta oru vakip paṟiyit taṭṭuvar. ivarka ḷukkut tālikaṭṭu campantam mutaliyaṉa uṇṭu. nākattāṉ kāk kaṭavuḷic cēvikkumpōtu ivarkaḷ pāmpaṉ tuḷḷu eṉṉum āṭal purivārkaḷ.
pirāki: ivarkaḷ vaṭa intiyāviṉiṉṟum vanta picci eṭukkum makkaḷ. pirākikaḷ matattil viṇavar; teṉkali nāmam iṭuvar; tuḷaci māli aṇivar.
poṇṭāri: ivarkaḷ kañcam pakutiyil vāḻum ōriyariṉ ampaṭṭar. ivarkaḷ tāy māmaṉ piḷḷiyi allatu tantiyiṉ cakōtariyiṉ piḷḷiyi maṇattal kūṭātu. kaliyāṇattukku muṉ ivarkaḷukku vil, ampu allatu cakaṭi marattōṭu pōlimaṇam naṭattappaṭum.
poṇṭāṉ: ivarkaḷ vaṭa maliyāḷa aracaṉiṉ pallakkuk kāvuvōr. ivarkaḷiṟ cilar māttiram kaḷḷik kōṭṭiyiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ naṭi, uṭi, pēccu mutaliyaṉa tamiḻarip pōṉṟavi. cātāraṇa tamiḻaṉit toṭuvatāl aracaṉuk kut tīṭṭu uṇṭākum. appoḻutu ivarkaḷ avaṉi araṇmaṉiyi liruntu ālayattukkum ālayattiliruntu araṇmaṉikkum cumantu celvar.
potuvāṉ: kōyiṟ kāval puriyum ampalavācikaḷ ip peyar peṟuvar. tiruvitāṅkūril potuvar eṉpatu maṟavaril oru piriviṉa rukkup peyar. peṇkaḷ tamatu cātiyiluḷḷa āṭavarōṭum pirāmaṇa rōṭum campantam vittukkoḷvar. āṭavar tamatu cātiyilum nāyarc cātiyilum campantam vikkalām. peṇkaḷ potu vicciyar allatu potuvattikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar.
polikar : itu pāḷayakkāraṉ eṉpataṟku iṉṉoru peyar. pāḷayakkārariṉ kīḻuḷḷavarkaḷum polikar eṉappaṭuvar.
poṟōcā: ivarkaḷ kañcam, vicākappaṭṭiṉaṅkaḷil vāḻum uḻavar.
maṅkalavar: ampaṭṭar maṅkalavar eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ tuḷuvar, tamiḻar eṉa iru piriviṉarāvar. ivarkaḷukkuḷ tirumaṇak kalappu illi. tirumaṇak kālattil nākacuram ūtutalāl ivarkaḷ maṅkalavar eṉappaṭuvar. kaliyāṇa kulam eṉpatuvum ivarkaḷukku maṟṟoru peyar.
maṇavāḷaṉ: ivarkaḷ nāyariṉ oru piriviṉar.
maṇiyakāraṉ: itaṟkuk kaṇkāṇippavaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ. itu cempaṭavaṉ, parivāraṅkaḷ eṉpōrukkum paṭṭappeyar. iṭiyaril oru vakuppiṉariyum itu kuṟikkum. ip peyar (māṭṭuk kuk kaṭṭum) maṇi eṉpatiliruntu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ic col maṇikar eṉat tirintu tamiḻnāṭṭil kirāmat talimikkāraṉik kuṟikkum.
maṇṭātāṉceṭṭi: ivarkaḷ citinta kaṉṉaṭa moḻi pēcuvar. makkaḷ tāyam koḷvar. ivarkaḷ vēnāṭṭil vāḻntārkaḷ. mantātaṉaṉ eṉpatu makāvali nāṭu eṉpataṉ tiripu. nellak kōṭṭik kum tīppakkāṭṭukkumiṭiyiluḷḷa pakutikku ip peyar iṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. pūppu aṭintapiṉ peṇkaḷukku maṇamākum. cila camayaṅ kaḷil maṇamakaṉ peṇṇip peṟuvataṟkup peṇṇiṉ tanti vīṭṭi liruntu oru āṇṭu mutal nāṉku āṇṭukaḷ vari vēli ceyvāṉ. maṇamāṉa peṇkaḷ kaṇavaṉiṉ cakōtararōṭu kūṭi vāḻtal kuṟṟa mākātu. iṟakkum taṟuvāyiluḷḷavarkaḷukkuc cōṟum poṉṉum iṭṭa ēṉattiliruntu ciṟitu nīrik kuṭikkak koṭup pārkaḷ.
maṇṭi: maṇṭik kaṟuppaṉi vaḻipaṭum kaḷḷar cātiyiṉar ip peyar peṟuvar.
maṇṭuvar: teluṅku nāṭṭil alintu tiriyum maruttuvar ip peyar peṟuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ maṉiviyar maruttuvaccikaḷākat toḻil purivar.
maṇṇāṭi: paḻani maliyil vāḻum kuṟavarukku ip peyar vaḻaṅkum.
maṇṇāṉ: itu tiruvitāṅkūr malic cātiyiṉarukkum vaḻaṅkum peyar. kuḻanti piṟantu oru āṇṭukkup piṉ kuṭumpattil mūttavar maṇi kōtta māliyik kuḻantiyiṉ kaḻuttiṟkaṭṭi ataṟkup peyariṭuvār. iṟantavar putikkappaṭuvar. piṇaṅkaḷip putikku muṉ avaṟṟiṉ vāyil ariciyiṭappaṭum. oru āṇṭiṉ piṉ iṟantavarukku uṇavu koṭukkappaṭum. maṇṇār tamiḻ pēcuvar. ivarkaḷukku ampaṭṭaṉum vaṇṇāṉum illi. urimi tāy vaḻi. maṇṇāṉeṉpatu vaṇṇāṉukkum peyarāka vaḻaṅkum.
matikar: ivarkaḷ cakkiliyarukkuc camamāṉa teluṅkar. ivar kaḷiṉ tēvarāṭṭi mātaṅki eṉappaṭuvāḷ. mātaṅki eṉṟum kaṉṉiyāka viruppāḷ. āṉāl, avaḷukkup pala piḷḷikaḷ iruppar. ivvā ṟiruttal kuṟṟamākak karutappaṭamāṭṭātu. mātaṅkiyiṉ āṇpāl ācāti. ivarkaḷ ellammāvip pukaḻntu pāṭuvar. ellammā elli eṉappaṭuvāḷ. avaḷ tīccuṭar pōla veḷippaṭuvāḷ. pirāmaṇar ellammāvi ilakkumi, kurammā, carasvati eṉa vaḻipaṭuvar. kiruṭṭiṇā mākāṇattil mātaṅkac ciṟuvar nūl pōṉṟa melliya vāri iṭattōḷ mītu pūṇūlāka aṇivar. mātaṅkar ūrammā eṉṉum teyvattiyum vaḻipaṭuvar. ūrvalattil mātaṅkat tēvaṭi yāṭkaḷ pāṭi āṭuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ caṭaṅkukaḷ piratāṉam eṉappaṭum. peṇkaḷ poṭṭut tāli aṇivar. talippūppuk kālattil peṇ ṇukkup pattunāḷ tīṭṭu uṇṭu. ivarkaḷ cāmpavar eṉappaṭuvārkaḷ.
marakkāyar: marakkāyar perumpālum paṟaṅkip pēṭṭiyiṟ kāṇappaṭuvar. ip peyar marak kalattik kuṟikkum marakkāpi eṉṉum arāpic colliṉ tiripu. ivarkaḷ intiyat tāymārukkum arāpiyat tantiyarukkum utittōr. cōṉakam eṉpatu arāpiyāvukku iṉṉoru peyar. intukkaḷāyiruntu marakkāyarāka māṟiyavarkaḷ puḷukkikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. marakkāyar arāpit tamiḻ pēcuvar. tamiḻi arāpi eḻuttukkāḷaleḻutuvar.
marāṭṭi allatu marācāri: marātti moḻi pēcuvōr marāṭṭikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. uṇmiyāṉa marāttikaḷ kōvāviliruntu vantavarkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ ciṟanta teyvam makātēvi. vitavikaḷ maṟumaṇam purivar. tañcāvūril irunta kaṭici marāṭṭiya aracaṉ civāci. ivaṉ 1855il maraṇamaṭintāṉ.
malacar: ivarkaḷ kōyamuttūr, kocci mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvar. kāṇiyāḷarukku aṭimikaḷ pōla vēlikaḷ purivar. tamiḻum maliyāḷamuṅ kalanta moḻi pēcuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ kuṭicikaḷ pati eṉappaṭum. kāḷi, maṇakkāṭāttā, māriyammā mutaliyaṉa ivar kaḷiṉ teyvaṅkaḷākum. ivarkaḷiṉ vītikaḷ, cālikaḷ eṉappaṭum. nilamuṭiyavaṉ maṇṇāṭi eṉappaṭuvāṉ. tirumaṇaṅkaḷ peṇ vīṭṭil naṭipeṟum. maṇamakaṉ peṇṇukkut tāli kaṭṭuvāṉ. mūppaṉ iruvariṉ kikaḷiyum cērttu vippāṉ. ivarkaḷ iṟantu pōṉa muṉṉōri vaḻipaṭuvar. avarkaḷukku uṇavu ēḻu ilikaḷil paṭikkappaṭum. talippūppu aṭinta peṇ taṉik kuṭiciyil ēḻunāḷ taṅkuvāḷ. kuṭiciyiṉ muṉṉāl nāḻiyum viḷakkum vikkap paṭum. avaḷ avaṟṟi valakkāli muṉṉē vittuk kaṭantu cel vāḷ. piṇaṅkaḷ mukam kīḻē irukkumpaṭi putikkappaṭum; cila camayaṅ kaḷil irukkum niliyilum putikkappaṭum. peṇkaḷ iṭatu kikaḷil māttiram vaḷiyalkaḷ aṇintirup pārkaḷ. iraṇṭu kikaḷilum aṇintāl paṟiyaṉ avaṟṟi uṭittu mūppaṉukku aṟivikka vēṇṭum.
malikkāraṉ : maliyāḷattil malikaḷil vāḻum uḻavar ip peyar peṟuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ talimikkāraṉ malimūttāṉ eṉap paṭuvaṉ.
maliyāḷi: maliyāḷi eṉpataṟku maliyil vāḻpavaṉ eṉpatu poruḷ. makamatiya āṭci toṭaṅkiya kālattil kāñcīpuratti ṉiṉṟum maliyāḷattiṟ ceṉṟu vāḻntōr maliyāḷikaḷ eṉappaṭu kiṉṟaṉar eṉṉum katiyuṇṭu. ivarkaḷ cevarōy (Sevaroy) mali kaḷil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. cevarōy maliyil vāḻum makkaḷ tammik kāñci maṇṭalam eṉak kūṟik koḷvar. ivarkaḷ civaṉ, viṭṭuṇu, māriyammaṉ, turupati mutaliya kaṭavuḷari vaḻipaṭuvar. cila kōyil kaḷil mikap paḻaṅkālak kallāyutaṅkaḷ vikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ivarkaḷ kōyilkaḷil cattiyañ ceytu vaḻakkukaḷit tīrttuk koḷvārkaḷ. poṅkal viḻāvukkup piṉ erutu āṭṭam eṉṉum viḻāvi ivarkaḷ naṭattuvārkaḷ. mika uyarnta kulattavaṉ kuru eṉa ppaṭuvāṉ. kirāmatti luḷḷa vaḻakkukaḷit tīrkka varumpōtu ivaṉ kutiri mītēṟi varu vāṉ. mēlē kuṭi piṭikkappaṭum. piṉṉāl vāttiyaṅkaḷ olikkum. kuru talimuṟiyāka varupavaṉ. pattuk kirāmaṅ kaḷukku oru talimik kāraṉiruppāṉ. avaṉ paṭṭakkāraṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. tirumaṇak kālat til mutiyavar aṟukampul, veṟṟilikaḷil pālittoṭṭu maṇamakka ḷiṉ taliyic cuṟṟuvar. maṇamakaṉ maṇamakaḷukkut tāli kaṭṭuvāṉ. kaliyāṇa nāḷ ilakkiṉam eṉappaṭum. kaṉṉiyiṉ maṇam kaliyāṇam eṉappaṭum. vitaviyiṉ maṇam ‘kaṭṭik kiṟatu’ eṉappaṭum. viyapicārak kuṟṟattukku kuru tīrttaṅ koṭuttup peṇṇic cuttañ ceyvār. ivarkaḷ iṟantavarip putippar. kuṭṭa viyātiyāḷaratu m, karuppiṇi kaḷatum uṭal cuṭappaṭum. peṇṇip peṟuvataṟku maṇamakaṉ peṇ ṇiṉ tanti vīṭṭiliruntu oru āṇṭāvatu paṇi ceyya vēṇṭum. kollimali maliyāḷikaḷ tiruvaraṅkattukku (cīraṅkattukku) māṭu nērntu viṭuvar. piṇattip putitta iṭattil alaric ceṭi naṭap paṭum. kōyil māṭukaḷ poli erutukaḷ eṉappaṭum. iṟanta kōyil māṭukaḷukku mariyāti ceyyappaṭum. vīṭṭil kōṭṭāṉ irukkāta paṭi mukaṭṭil puṟpiṭikaḷ niṟutti vikkappaṭṭirukkum. maḻi vēṇṭu māyiṉ oruvar maṟṟavar mītu cāṇi uṇṭikaḷi eṟivar. cāṇiyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa piḷḷi yāri erukkumpattuḷ putippar. maḻi vanta vuṭaṉ piḷḷiyāri eṭuppar. taliyiṭi nōy kaṇṭōr teyvat tukkuc civappuc cēval nē rntuviṭuvar. pal neruṭukiṟavarkaḷ kōyiṟ piracātatti uṇpar. tirucciṉāppaḷḷi maliyāḷikaḷ tāyiṉ cakō tariyiṉ piḷḷiyi maṇappar. iv vaḻakkiṉāl ciṟuvaṉ pirāyamaṭinta peṇṇi maṇakka nērum. kollimalip peṇkaḷ piṟa āṭavarōṭu vāḻalām. peṟum piḷḷikaḷ kaṇavaṉukkuriyaṉavākak karutappaṭum. iru pālārum pala maṇaṅkaḷ ceyvar. maliyāḷi eṉpatu mali nāṭṭa varukkum peyarākum.
maliyāṉ: ivarkaḷ vaṭamaliyāḷattiṟ kāṇappaṭum oru kūṭṭattiṉar. ivarkaḷukku nōy vantāl enta mūrttiyāl (teyvat tāl) nērnta nōy eṉac cōtiṭaṉāl aṟintu tīrttu eṉṉum oruvakip pēyk kūttu āṭappaṭum. ucca veḷi eṉṉum iṉṉoru kiriyiyum ceyvārkaḷ. ik kiriyiyil nōyāḷi pōliyāka uyiruṭaṉ putikkappaṭuvāṉ. maliyāḷikaḷ periyaṇṇaṉiyum, pattirakāḷiyiyum vaḻipaṭuvar. appoḻutu veḷippāṭu kūṟappaṭum. pēyk kūttukkaḷiloṉṟu niṇaveḷi eṉappaṭum. ilaṅkic ciṅkaḷavarum nōyip pōkkuvataṟkup pēyāṭṭam āṭuvar.
malparayaṉ: ivarkaḷ malikaḷil vāḻum maṇṇāṉilum mēlāṉa cātiyiṉar. ivarkaḷuḷ makkaḷ tāyamum uṇṭu. ivarkaḷ moḻi maliyāḷattiṉ citivu. maṇamakaṉum maṇamakaḷum oru iliyiliruntu uṇṭapiṉ tāli kaṭṭappaṭum. piṟapput tīṭṭu tantikku oru mātamum, tāykku ēḻu nāḷum uṇṭu. iṟantavarkaḷi aṭakkañ ceyta iṭattil ivarkaḷ kal vippar.
maḷavarāyaṉ: itu ampalakkārariṉ paṭṭappeyar.
maṟavar: ivarkaḷ maturi, tirunelvēli, vaṭirāmanātapuram mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷum kaḷḷar vakuppiṉarip pōlap pirāmaṇariṉ toṭarpu ciṟitum illātavar. maṟavar eṉṉum col maṟam eṉṉum aṭiyākap piṟantatu. kaḷḷariṉ uṭpirivil maṟavarum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. maṟavariṉ talivaṉ cētupati eṉappaṭu pavaṉ. maṟavar perumpālum aracariṉ kīḻ pōrvīrarāka viruntaṉar. ivarkaḷiṟ perumpālōr ippoḻutu uḻutoḻil ceykiṉṟaṉar. muṉpu ivarkaḷiṟ palar māṭu tiruṭuvōrāka viruntaṉar. tirumaṇattil kaṇavaṉiṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāḷ peṇṇukkut tāli kaṭṭuvāḷ. ataṉpiṉ caṅku ūtappaṭum. peṇkaḷ kuḻavi iṭal eṉṉum pāṭṭup pāṭu vārkaḷ. maṇattukku muṉpu maṇamakaṉiṉ iḷiya cakōtari peṇ vīṭṭukku c ceṉṟu avaḷ kaḻuttil tāli kaṭṭip peṇṇi vīṭṭukku aḻittuc celvāḷ. cila mātaṅkaḷiṉ piṉ maṇakkiriyi naṭi peṟum. pala piḷḷikaḷ piṟantapiṉ maṇakkiriyi naṭappatumuṇṭu. peṇkaḷ maṟumaṇañ ceyvārkaḷ. peṇ pūppaṭinta ceytiyi vaṇṇāṉ uṟaviṉarukkuk kūṟuvāṉ. 16ām nāḷ avarkaḷukku muḻuk kāṭṭappaṭum. irāmanātapurattuc cempu nāṭṭu maṟavar akampaṭi yārit tamatu vēlikkārarākak karutuvar. iṟantavaṉukku akampaṭi yāṉē koḷḷik kuṭattic cuṭalikkuk koṇṭu celvāṉ. piṇak kuḻiyi āṇṭi tōṇṭuvāṉ. piṇam cāmi vikkappaṭum. maṟava riṭiyē piṇattic cuṭum vaḻakkamum uṇṭu. piṇattic cumantu celpavar nila pāvāṭi mītu naṭantu pōvar. mūṉṟāvatu nāḷ maṇṇiṉāl iliṅkam piṭittu vi ttu iliṅkattukkum, iṟantavar kaḷukkum, kākkikaḷukkum paliyiṭappaṭum. 16ām nāḷ piṇattip putitta iṭattil navatāṉiyam vitikkappaṭum. calli kaṭṭutal eṉ ṉum viḷiyāṭṭu maṟavarkaḷukkuḷ naṭipeṟum. atu ippoḻutu araciṉarāl taṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kuṟikkappaṭṭa oru tiṉattil mūrkka muḷḷa erutukaḷ kaḷarikkuk koṇṭu varappaṭum. avaṟṟiloṉṟi aviḻttu viṭṭāl atu niṟkum kūṭṭattiṟ pāyntu oruvaṉit turattic cellum. appoḻutu avaṉ nilattil viḻuntu paṭuttuk koḷvāṉ. appoḻutu atu iṉṉoruvaṉit turattic cellum. makkaḷ āravārañ ceytu koṇṭu ōṭuvārkaḷ. maṟavar vēṭṭi yāṭumpōtu vaḷitaṭiyip payaṉpaṭuttuvar. atu ilakkiṟpaṭṭu eṟintavaṉiṭattukku t tirumpi varum. kāḷi, kaṟuppaṉ, mūtta kaṟuppaṉ, periya kaṟuppaṉ, maturi vīraṉ, aiyaṉār, muṉicāmi mutaliyōr ivar kaḷiṉ teyvaṅkaḷāvar. maṟavap peṇkaḷ kātit tuḷiyiṭṭum tuḷiyip periyatākkuvar. maṟavariṉ paṭṭappeyar tēvaṉ, talivaṉ, cērvikkāraṉ, kariyāṉ, irācavamcam eṉpaṉa.
mastāṉ: itu makamatiya ñāṉikaḷukku vaḻaṅkum paṭṭappeyar.
māṅkalyam: itu mārāṉkaḷiṉ uṭpirivu. ivarkaḷ nāyariṉ tāli kaṭṭuk kaliyāṇattil aṭṭamaṅkalaṅkaḷik koṇṭu celvar. arici, nel, teṉṉaṅkuṟuttu, ampu, mukam pārkkum kaṇṇāṭi, veṇtukil, ceppu mutaliyaṉa aṭṭamaṅkalattilaṭaṅkum.
mātaṅkar: itu matiṅkarukku iṉṉoru peyar. matiṅkar tammi mātaṅka makkaḷ eṉavuṅ kūṟuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ teyvam mātaṅki. mātaṅka rāl mariyāti ceyyappaṭum tēvaṭiyāṭkaḷukkum mātaṅkar eṉap peyaruṇṭu.
mātavaṉ: itu nāyariṉ uṭpiriviṉarākiya puvikkāppaṇikka riṉ paṭṭappeyar.
māppiḷḷimār: ivarkaḷ maliyāḷattiluḷḷa kalappu makamatiyar. ivarkaḷiṉ tantiyar arāpiyar. tāyar tirāviṭar. ivarkaḷ ki.pi. oṉpatām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal maliyāḷattil perukat toṭaṅki ṉārkaḷ. patiṉintām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāskōṭakāmā kaḷḷikkōṭ ṭikku vantapōtu māppiḷḷimār aracāṅkattil atikāram peṟṟiruntārkaḷ. tippuccultāṉ maliyāḷatti āṇṭa kālattil palar makamatiya matattukku māṟṟappaṭṭārkaḷ. itaṉāl ivarkaḷiṉ eṇ atikappaṭṭatu. matam māṟṟappaṭṭavarkaḷ perumpālum mukku var allatu kariyārāvar. māppiḷḷi eṉṉum col marumakaṉ allatu maṇamakaṉ eṉṉum poruḷ tarum. ip peyar maliyāḷattiṟ kuṭiyēṟi maliyāḷikaḷi maṇanta makamatiyar, kiṟittuvar, yūtarkaḷik kuṟittatu. itu ippoḻutu makamatiyari māttiram kuṟikkiṉṟatu. ivarkaḷ perumpālum marumakkaḷ tāyam uṭiyavarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ makamatiya caṭṭattiṉpaṭi naṭappar. perum pālum pala peṇkaḷi maṇappar. ilakkat tīvukaḷilum māppiḷḷi mār kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ aṅku kōyar, mālumi, urukkāraṉ, takkuru, milikkaṉ eṉappaṭuvar.
mārāṉ allatu mārāyaṉ: ivarkaḷ maliyāḷattil mēḷamaṭippōr. vaṭamaliyāḷattil ivarkaḷ ōccar eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ uyarnta nāyar kuṭumpattiṉarukku ampaṭṭarākac cēvippar. kōṭṭayam, kurumpira nāṭṭut tālukkākkaḷil ivarkaḷ nāvitar, mēḷakārar āvar. nāyar iḻavu vīṭukaḷil purōkitarākac cēvippar. ivarkaḷ peṇkaḷōṭu pirāmaṇar campantam vittukkoḷvar. vaṭatiruvitāṅkūril ivarkaḷ māṅkaliyam eṉavum paṭuvar. ivarkaḷuk kuk kuruppu, paṇikkar mutaliya paṭṭappeyarkaḷuṇṭu. ivarkaḷil oru nūl, irunūl eṉṉum iru piriviṉaruṇṭu. oru nūleṉpatu vāḻ nāḷil oru kaliyāṇam māttiram ceytukoḷḷum pirivu. ivarkaḷ acuppāṇikaḷ eṉavum paṭuvar. uyarnta maṟavarukku āṟu urimika ḷuṇṭu. avi pāṇō(paṇ) , cōṇi(pāṭi), tirumuṟṟam(kōyil muṟṟam perukkal) veḷic cōṟu(pēykaḷukku veḷiyē vikkappaṭum paliccōṟu), puccōṟu(teyvattukku vikkappaṭuñ cōṟu) eṉpaṉa. iḻavu vīṭṭil eḷpōṭum kiriyi avaṉāl ceyyappaṭukiṟatu. maliyāḷattil vāttiyaṅkaḷ maram eṉappaṭum. timili caṅku, ceṇ kulam, ceṉṭa(ṭu) mutaliyavaṟṟi oruṅkē olittal pāṇi koṭṭuku eṉappaṭum.
mārvāṭi: mārvāṭā tēcattavar mārvāṭi allatu mārvāri eṉappaṭum. ivarkaḷ perumpālum civamatattiṉar.
mālar: ivarkaḷ teluṅku nāṭṭup paṟiyar, mātaṅkarkaḷik kuṟikkum. ivarkaḷukku ampaṭṭar, vāttiyakārar, tēvaṭiyāṭkaḷ uṇṭu. aṅkammā, pettammā mutaliyaṉa ivarkaḷ teyvaṅkaḷ. mālariṉ mukkiya toḻil necavu. tirumaṇam maṇamakaḷ vīṭṭil nikaḻum.
mālumi: ilakkat tīvukaḷil marakkalamōṭṭum mukamatiyar mālumikaḷeṉappaṭuvar.
māli(māli): ivarkaḷ tām muṉ kāciyil vāḻntu piṉ ceyappūr aracaric cēvippataṟku vantārkaḷ eṉak kūṟuvar. pūppaṭiyumuṉ peṇkaḷ maṇamuṭippar. vitavikaḷ maṟumaṇañ ceyvar. māli eṉpataṟku māli kaṭṭikaḷ eṉpatu poruḷ. ivarkaḷ kōyilkaḷil vēli purivārkaḷ. oriyamoḻi pēcuvārkaḷ.
māṉikaṭṭāḷ: itu tēvatācikku iṉṉoru peyar.
mītārakkāraṉ: ivar teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭa, oriya tamiḻ nāṭukaḷil mūṅkiṟ kūṭi, pāy mutaliyaṉa muṭikiṉṟavarkaḷāvar. ivarkaḷ vīrullu (maṇamākātu iṟanta vālipar), pērāṇṭālu (maṇamākātu iṟanta peṇkaḷ allatu kaṇavaṉukku muṉ iṟantavarkaḷ) eṉpōri vaḻipaṭuvar. ivarkaḷuḷ civavaḻipāṭṭiṉar iṟantōric camāti vippar; viṇavar cuṭuvar. ivarkaḷ iyantiraṅkaḷi eḻuti vittu aṭitta tāyattukaḷi aṇivar. vitavikaḷ tāliyum kāppum aṇiyamāṭṭārkaḷ. kāliṉ iraṇṭāvatu viralil aṇiyum meṭṭu eṉṉum mōtiramum aṇiyārkaḷ.
mīlatēvar: ivarkaḷ teṉkaṉṉaṭattut tēvaṭiyāṭkaḷāvar.
mīṉōṉ: itu camariṉ (vaṭamaliyāḷa aracaṉ) taṉatu eḻut tāḷaṉukkuk koṭukkum paṭṭappeyar. ippoḻutu atu nāyaril oru piriviṉarukkup peyarāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. maliyāḷattil kirāmak kaṇakkaṉ (karṇam) mīṉōṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. ip paṭṭam kocci aracaṉāl palarukkuk koṭukkappaṭṭatu. itu teṟkē vaḻaṅkum piḷḷippaṭṭattukkuc camam. mīṉōṉ paṭṭam koṭuttapiṉ avaṉukku ōliyum eḻuttāṇiyum koṭukkappaṭukiṟatu. ip paṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷiṉ peṇvaḻiyār māttiram ip paṭṭattip payaṉ paṭuttalām. mīṉōṉ eṉpatu pōṉahīta irāv. irāv eṉpatu marāṭṭip paṭṭappeyar.
mukatōrar: ivarkaḷ koṇṭatōrariṉ oru piriviṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ moḻi teluṅku. aṇṇā, aiyā, tōrā eṉpaṉa ivar kaḷiṉ paṭṭappeyar. ivarkaḷil cūriyavamicam, nākavamicam eṉa iru pirivukaḷuṇṭu. oruvaṉ tāymāmaṉ makaḷi maṇakkalām. maṇamakaṉ, maṇamakaḷ eṉṉum iruvariṉ viralkaḷiyum tāymāmaṉ cērttu vippāṉ.
mukkuvaṉ: mukkuvar maliyāḷak kaṭalkaḷil mīṉ piṭippavarka ḷāvar. tāḻnta vakuppiṉarukku ivarkaḷ pallakkuc cumappar. ōṭakkāra rākavum toḻil ceyvar. parampariyāka varum ivarkaḷiṉ talivaṉ ariyaṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ. ivarkaḷiṉ mukkiya teyvam pattirakāḷi. ivarkaḷ kulattil oruvaṉ pūcāriyāka viruppāṉ. pirāmaṇar kōyil kaḷil ivarkaḷ nuḻital kūṭātu. vaṭamaliyāḷattil ivarkaḷukku marumakkaḷ tāyamum, teṟkil makkaḷ tāyamum uṇṭu. ivarkaḷiṉ mukkiya toḻilkaḷ cuṇṇāmpuc cūḷivippatu, mañcaḷ cumappatu mutaliyaṉa. mañcaḷ eṉpatu taṭiyil kaṭṭappaṭṭa oru vaki ūñcal mītu āḷi vittuc cumattal. ivarkaḷ ilaṅkiyiliruntu ceṉṟavarkaḷeṉak karutappaṭuvar. mukkuvar tīyarilum tāḻntōr. ivar kaḷiṟ palar mēlnilikku vantuḷḷaṉar. ivarkaḷiṟ poṉṉillam, cempillam, kārillam, kāccillam eṉa nāṉku pirivukaḷuṇṭu. ivar kaḷuḷ kāvuttīyaṉ allatu maṇimakaṉ eṉṉum pirivu muṇṭu. ivarkaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷukku mayirviṉiyum ceyvar. ivarkaḷiṉ caṅkaṅkaḷ irācciyam eṉappaṭum. periyavarkaḷ kaṭavaṉ eṉappaṭuvārkaḷ. talivaṉ arayaṉ allatu karaṇavaṉ eṉappaṭuvaṉ. karaṇavaṉ araca ṉāl teriyappaṭuvāṉ. ivarkaḷukku mūṭṭappaṭṭa ōlikkuṭi, taṭi, arikkuk kaṭṭum civapputtuṇi mutaliya aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷuṇṭu. veḷippāṭu kūṟuvōr āyattaṉ allatu attaṉ eṉappaṭuvar. āyattaṉ eṉpatu āyutattaṉ eṉpataṉ marūu ākalām. urukkoḷ pavaṉ allatu teyvamēṟupavaṉ vāḷi vittiruppāṉ. peṇkaḷ pūppaṭintapiṉ maṇam muṭikkappaṭuvar. karuppavatikku ēḻāvatu mātam puḷikkuṭi allatu neykkuṭi eṉṉum caṭaṅku naṭattappaṭum. kuḻanti piṟakkum variyum kaṇavaṉ tāṭi vaḷarppāṉ. piḷḷi piṟantapiṉ mūṉṟāvatu nāḷ mayirviṉi ceytu koḷvāṉ. tīṭṭu ēḻu nāṭkaḷuk kuṇṭu. iṟantavaṉiṉ mūttamakaṉ āṟu mātaṅkaḷukku mayirviṉi ceytu koḷḷa māṭṭāṉ.
mucāttu: ivarkaḷ maliyāḷattiluḷḷa mūttatukaḷāvar. ivarkaḷ ampalavācikaḷilum uyarntōr. nampi, nampiyār eṉṉum paṭṭaṅkaḷ ivarkaḷukku uṇṭu. ivarkaḷiṉ peṇkaḷ maṇa ammā mār eṉappaṭuvar. iḷiyatukaḷum mūttatukaḷum maliyāḷattil nayiṉākkaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. mūttatukaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷ maṭam, illam eṉap paṭum. nampūtirikaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷukkum ip peyarkaḷ uṇṭu. tirumaṇat tukku muṉ peṇkaḷ tiruvaḷiyamum, kuḻalumaṇivar. viḻākkālaṅ kaḷil palakkā vaḷiyamaṇivar. tirumaṇattukkup piṉ kātil cūṭṭum, kaḻuttil tāliyumaṇivar. vitavikaḷ cūṭṭu māttiramaṇivar. mūttatukaḷ uṭkōyilkaḷiṉ paṭikaḷik kaḻuvuvar. vikkirakaṅkaḷip pātukāttuk koḷvar. kōyiṟ piracātam mutaliyavaṟṟik koṇṭu vāḻvar. ivarkaḷiṉ kuṭumpattil mūttavaṉ māttiram maṇam ceytu koḷvaṉ. maṟṟavarkaḷ ampalavācip peṇkaḷic campantam vippar. āṇkaḷ nāṉku peṇkaḷ variyil maṇakkalām. mūtta makaṉukkup pāṭṭaṉiṉ peyariṭappaṭum. iraṇṭāvatu makaṉukkut tāyvaḻippāṭṭaṉiṉ peyariṭappaṭum. pūṇūlaṇital ēḻu vayatu mutal patiṉoru vayatukkiṭiyil naṭipeṟum. ivarkaḷukku maraṇattīṭṭu pattu nāḷ. kōyiluḷ iruntu ivarkaḷ uṇṇalām. mūttatukaḷ ampalavācikaḷilum uyarntōrum, iḷiyatukaḷ pirāmaṇarukkut tāḻntōrumāvar. mucāttu mūttatu eṉṉum peyarkaḷ akappotu val eṉṉum peyarōṭu oṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉa. taṭampu mītu kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvuruvam vittu vītivalam ceyyappaṭum. taṭampu eṉpatu kēṭakam pōṉṟu kaviḻnta taṭṭu. aṭikaḷ eṉpōrum piṭārarum oruvareṉat terikiṟatu. piṭārar pūṇūlaṇiyātu kōyiṟ pūci ceyvar.
muṭavāṇṭi: ivarkaḷ koṅkaṇa vēḷāḷaril oru piriviṉar. āṇṭi eṉpataṟkup paramparip piccikkārar eṉpatu poruḷ.
mutuvar: ivarkaḷ kōyamputtūr, maturi, maliyāḷam pakutikaḷil kāṇappaṭum uḻavarākiya maliccātiyiṉar, ivarkaḷ maṟṟavarkaḷit takappaṉmār eṉpar. ivarkaḷukkiṭiyil kañcaṉ, kaṟuppuk kuñci, kuñcita, kārmēkam mutaliya peyarkaḷ peritum vaḻaṅkum. kaṟuppāyi, kūppi, pēycci mutaliya peyarkaḷ peṇkaḷukku vaḻaṅkum. kaṭiciyākap peṟum āṇ piḷḷikaḷukku irāmaṉ, ilakkumaṇaṉ eṉṟum, iraṭṭip peṇkaḷukku ilaṭcumi, irāmi eṉṟum peyariṭappaṭum. ivarkaḷiṉ talimikkāraṉ mēlvākaṉ eṉavum, utavi atikāri mūppaṉ eṉavum paṭuvar. ivarkaḷukku marumakkaḷ tāyamuṇṭu. nimittaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷukku nampikkiyuṇṭu. oruvaṉ māmaṉ makaḷi maṇakkalām. matam campantamāṉa caṭaṅkukaḷukku ivarkaḷ kurumāri aḻippatilli. iṟantavarkaḷiṉ mukam pārkkum paṭiyākap piṇattip putippar. tīttaṭṭik kaṟkaḷālum, irumpālum tī mūṭṭuvar. karuṅkuraṅkiṉ iṟicciyi uṇpar. iruḷarum mutuvarum malippakkaṅkaḷil toṅkum tēṉ kūṭukaḷiliruntu tēṉeṭuppar.
muttiriyaṉ: pāḷayakkārarkaḷukku ip peyar vaḻaṅkum. itu teluṅkil muttirācaṉ eṉa vaḻaṅkum. it teluṅkuc cātiyiṉar kiruṭṭiṇā, vaṭa ārkkāṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷiṉ paṭṭap peyarkaḷ tōrāvum, nāyuṭuvum. ivarkaḷ īcal kaḷip piṭittu vaṟṟaliṭṭu pāṉikaḷil cēmittu vittu avaṟṟi uṇavākap payaṉpaṭuttuvar. ivarkaḷukkup piṟapput tīṭṭu pattu nāḷ.
mūtāṉ: ivarkaḷ maliyāḷattiluḷḷa vāṇikam puriyum vakuppiṉar. peṇkaḷ ceṭṭiccikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷ nāyariṉ paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷip piṉpaṟṟuvar. pālakkāṭu, vaḷḷuvanāṭu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil ivarkaḷ perumpālum kāṇappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷiṟ cilar tamakku eḻuttaccaṉ eṉṉum paṭṭappeyari vittu vaḻaṅkuvar. ivarkaḷukku marumakkaḷ tāyamuṇṭu. ivarkaḷiṉ tīṭṭuk kaḻippu koṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉum purōkitaṉāṟ ceyyappaṭum.
meykāval: itu paṇṭāraṅkaḷukku oru peyar. ivarkaḷ kaṭavuḷiṉ meyyik kāppavarkaḷ.
mēstiri: itu pōrccukkēya (Meytro) eṉṉum col. itu intiya nāṭṭu moḻikaḷil ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. mēstiri eṉpa taṟkut tiṟamiyāṉa vēliyāḷ eṉpatu poruḷ. itu tamiḻnāṭṭil cemmārukkum vēṟu cila toḻilāḷarukkum peyarāka vaḻaṅkum.
mēḷakkāraṉ: ivarkaḷ vāttiyakkārar. tōriya mēḷakkāraṉ periya mēḷa cēvi māttiram cēvippaṉ. tamiḻ mēḷakkārar tēvaṭi yāṭkaḷōṭu campantappaṭṭa ciṉṉa mēḷa cēvaṉiyum ceyvar. tēvaṭiyāḷiṉ makaḷ tāy ceytu vanta toḻiliyē ceyvāḷ. naṭṭuvaṉ eṉpōr tēvaṭiyāṭkaḷukku naṭaṉam paḻakkuvōr.
moṇṭi: ilaṇṭā, kallaṭiccittaṉ, kallaṭimaṅkaṉ eṉpaṉa orē kūṭṭattiṉarik kuṟikkum peyarkaḷ. ivarkaḷ picci eṭukkum paramparip paṇṭāraṅkaḷ. picciyiṭāviṭṭāl ivarkaḷ tamatu toṭiyi veṭṭuvārkaḷ; kallil taliyi uṭippārkaḷ; vānti eṭuppārkaḷ.
moyili: ivarkaḷ teṉkaṉṉaṭattil kōyilkaḷil vēli purivōr. peṇkaḷ tamatu kaṇavarōṭu vāḻa virumpāviṭilum, vitavikaḷ maṟumaṇam ceyya muṭiyāmaliruntālum avarkaḷ kōyilukkuc ceṉṟu kōyiṟ palic cōṟṟil cila uṇṭikaḷ peṟṟu uṇpārkaḷ. piṉpu avarkaḷ araciṉar uttiyōkattariṭam koṇṭu pōkappaṭuvar. avarkaḷ avvāṟu ceyyat tuṇintamikkuk kāraṇam vicārikkappaṭum. pirāmaṇap peṇkaḷāyiṉ kōyiliṉuḷ irukka aṉumatikkappaṭuvārkaḷ. avarkaḷukkut tiṉam uṇavum āṇṭil oru tuṇiyum kiṭikkum. avarkaḷ kōyilip perukkavum cāmari vīcavum vēṇṭum. avarkaḷ perumpālum pirāmaṇariṉ allatu uttiyākattariṉ vippāṭṭikaḷāka viruppārkaḷ. avarkaḷ peṟum piḷḷikaḷ moyir eṉappaṭuvar. avarkaḷ nūlaṇintu kōyilip perukkik kōyiṟ paṇiviṭi ceyvar. avarkaḷ tēvaṭikar eṉavum paṭuvar.
morācu: ivarkaḷ micūril kāṇappaṭuvar. maṇam pēcum paruvam vantatum peṇkaḷ valakkiyiṉ mūṉṟām nālām viralkaḷi veṭṭiviṭuvar. iv vaḻakku āstirēliya, polinīciya, amerikkap paḻaṅkuṭikaḷiṭiyum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
mōker: ivarkaḷ tuḷuppēcum teṉ kaṉṉaṭa mīṉpiṭikkārar. ivarkaḷiṉ kuṭiyiruppu paṭṭiṉameṉappaṭum. ceṉṉiyiluḷḷa mīṉ piṭikkārar paṭṭaṇavar eṉappaṭuvar. ivarkaḷiṉ talimikkāraṉ kuṟikkāraṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. ip patavi talimuṟiyāka varuvatu. ivar kaḷiṉ urimi peṇvaḻi. kēvā eṉṉum tuḷu ampaṭṭaṉ ivarka ḷukkuc cirikka māṭṭāṉ; koṅkaṇi ampaṭṭar cirippar. kuḻanti piṟantu ēḻāvatu nāḷ vaṇṇātti kuḻantiyiṉ ariyil nūl kaṭṭip peyariṭuvāḷ. ip peyar cilanāṭkaḷiṉ piṉ kiviṭappaṭum. piṉ vēṟu peyariṭappaṭum. ivarkaḷiṉ paṭṭappeyar marakkāvēru.
yūtar: koccittīvil kaṟuppu yūtar, veḷḷi yūtar eṉa iru vaki yūtarkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ neṭunāṭkaḷukku muṉ palastīṉ nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum vantōrāvar. pāskara iravivarmaṉ eṉṉum aracaṉ muciṟik kōṭṭil vāḻnta yūtaṉoruvaṉukku aḷitta paṭṭiya moṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. atu av varacaṉiṉ āṭciyil 36vatu āṇṭu yōcēp irappāṉ añcuvaṇṇaṉ eṉṉum yūtaṉukku aḷikkappaṭṭatu. ivaṉukkuk koṭukkappaṭṭa urimikaḷ varumāṟu: avaṉ aintu koṭikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttalām. ulāvac cellumpōtu vēliyāṭkaḷ tīpam piṭittuc cellalām. yāṉi kutirikaḷil ēṟic cellalām. aracarippōla pavaṉi varalām. pakaṟkālattil tīvart tiyip payaṉpaṭuttalām. palavaki vāttiyaṅkaḷ, periya mēṉam mutaliyavaṟṟi olippikka urimiyuṇṭu. nila pāvāṭiyiṟ cella lām. aracarippōl mēṟkaṭṭiyiṉ kīḻ irukkalām. irappāṉiṉ kīḻuḷḷa eḻupattiraṇṭu kuṭumpaṅkaḷum avaṉukkuk kīḻ aṭaṅki naṭakka vēṇṭum. ic cācaṉam kaliyukam 3481il (ki.pi.370l) eḻutap paṭṭatu. yūtar cālamaṉ aracaṉ kālam mutal (ki.mu. 900) maliyāḷak karikaḷukku vantu vāṇikam purintārkaḷ. ki.mu. 6ām nūṟṟāṇṭil ciracu (Cyrus) eṉṉum pāracīka aracaṉiṉkīḻ aṭimikaḷāka vāḻa virumpāta yūtamakkaḷ intiyāvil vantu kuṭiyēṟiṉar.
haṉṭar (E.W.Hunter) yūtar ki.pi. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭukku muṉ intiyāviṟ kuṭiyēṟiyiruntārkaḷ eṉak kūṟiyuḷḷār. ceṅkaṭali luḷḷa mēōs, hēmasiliruntu arāpiyar ilaṅki, maliyāḷam mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkuc ceṉṟa ōr urōmāṉiyar maliyāḷattil oru yūtar kuṭiyiruppik ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kaṇṭār uviṣ (Wish) eṉpār. tōmas ñāṉiyār ki.pi. 5il intiyāvi aṭintā reṉṟum yūtar ki.pi. 69il intiyāvukkuc ceṉṟaṉar eṉṟum kūṟiyuḷḷār.
